*Xuechen Zhang,Zijian Huang,Kai Yang,Weijia Zhang,Jiasi Chen,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出向量引导策略优化（VSPO），通过在GRPO中引入行为控制向量来采样不同强度的行为轨迹，解决多目标优化中稀疏行为奖励瓶颈问题。VSPO可视为一种在策略潜在自蒸馏过程，通过上采样稀有行为丰富轨迹多样性，在理论和实验上均优于传统奖励塑形方法，在MATH、MMLU-Pro等推理基准上实现了目标行为控制与任务准确率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型需要同时优化主要准确性目标和次要行为偏好（如冗长度、亲和度、技术专业性），但基模型往往极少甚至完全不展现目标行为，形成稀疏行为奖励瓶颈。现有方法难以有效处理这种多目标优化问题。

Method: VSPO通过修改GRPO，引入与目标行为关联的引导向量来控制生成轨迹的行为强度，以不同引导强度采样轨迹，实现对稀有行为的上采样和轨迹多样性的丰富。该过程可解释为一种在策略潜在自蒸馏机制，使模型内化其引导向量。

Result: 在MATH和MMLU-Pro等多个推理基准上，针对解释专业性、置信度表达、对误导上下文的鲁棒性、响应冗长度四种目标行为，VSPO在保持或提升任务准确率的同时，持续改善了目标行为控制能力，优于奖励塑形、教师轨迹蒸馏和基于引导的基线方法。

Conclusion: VSPO通过向量引导采样机制有效缓解了稀疏奖励问题，在理论上证明了比奖励塑形GRPO更优的迭代复杂度，实践上实现了目标行为与任务准确率的更好权衡，为语言模型的多目标对齐提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Modern language models often need to optimize a primary accuracy objective while also accommodating secondary behavioral preferences, such as verbosity, agreeableness, or the level of technical expertise in its response. In practice, a base model may exhibit a desired behavior very rarely or not at all. Thus, endowing the model with a target behavior creates a sparse behavioral reward bottleneck. To address such multi-objective problems, we introduce Vector-Steered Policy Optimization (VSPO) which employs a steering vector associated with the target behavior to control the behavior intensity of the generated rollouts. VSPO is obtained by modifying GRPO to sample rollouts with varying steering intensities. This process can be interpreted as an on-policy latent self-distillation procedure where the model internalizes its steering vector. By varying steering intensities, VSPO upsamples rare behaviors and enriches rollout diversity, which alleviates the sparse reward issue and provably accelerates the policy optimization. Through comprehensive theory and experiments, we establish that VSPO has favorable properties compared to vanilla reward shaping and other alternative approaches. Specifically, under a bandit abstraction, VSPO provably achieves better iteration complexity than reward-shaped GRPO when the steering-induced distributions are sufficiently aligned with the target behavior. We evaluate VSPO across multiple reasoning benchmarks, including MATH and MMLU-Pro, for four target behaviors: explanation expertise, confidence expression, robustness to misleading context, and response verbosity. Our results show that VSPO consistently improves the control along target behavior while maintaining or improving task accuracy compared with reward shaping, teacher-trace distillation, and guidance-based baselines.

</details>


### [109] [Position: Zeroth-Order Optimization in Deep Learning Is Underexplored, Not Underpowered](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15622)
*Sijia Liu,Yicheng Lang,Soumyadeep Pal,Changsheng Wang,Yancheng Huang,Chongyu Fan,James Diffenderfer,Bhavya Kailkhura,Yihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出零阶优化在深度学习中被低估，其局限性源于短视的开发实践（如全空间、逐元素、以估计器为中心的设计），并提出了六个立场，从算法、系统和评估三个层面重新审视ZO优化的可行性与独特优势，主张围绕其特性（前向计算、通信高效、资源友好）进行大规模、系统感知的资源高效学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零阶优化常因估计器方差大和查询复杂度高而被认为无法扩展，本文认为这一结论不准确，源于开发实践的短视，旨在纠正对ZO优化的误解，挖掘其未被充分探索的潜力。

Method: 提出六个立场：1) 重新审视以估计器为中心的ZO方法的可行性边界（方差控制、方差-查询权衡、方向导数视角）；2) 子空间与谱视角实现可解释的方差缩减和优雅的查询缩放；3) 前向计算特性在通信高效、流水线友好和资源受限训练中的系统优势；4) 去混淆ZO评估与任务复杂性。

Result: 论证了ZO优化并非本质上不可扩展，而是被低估；识别出子空间/谱方法、前向计算系统优势以及评估去混淆等未被充分探索的机会。

Conclusion: 主张围绕ZO优化的独特优势重新思考其发展方向，为大规模、系统感知、资源高效的学习开辟可行路径。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization, learning from finite differences of function evaluations without backpropagation, has recently regained attention in deep learning due to its memory efficiency and applicability to gray- or black-box pipelines. Yet, ZO methods are often dismissed as fundamentally unscalable because of estimator variance and unfavorable query complexity. We argue that this conclusion might be misguided: ZO optimization is underexplored, not underpowered. We show that many perceived limitations stem from myopic development practices, most notably full-space, element-wise, estimator-centric designs. We articulate six positions spanning the algorithmic, systems, and evaluation stack. First, we revisit the feasibility boundaries of estimator-centric ZO methods through variance control, variance-query tradeoffs, and directional-derivative lenses. Then, we identify three underexplored opportunities: (i) subspace and spectral views of ZO that enable interpretable variance reduction with graceful query scaling, (ii) the forward-only nature of ZO as a systems advantage for communication-efficient, pipeline-friendly, and resource-constrained training, and (iii) the need to de-obfuscate ZO evaluations from task complexity. We strongly advocate rethinking ZO optimization around its unique strengths and acting accordingly, opening a viable path toward large-scale, system-aware, and resource-efficient learning with ZO optimization.

</details>


### [110] [IO-SVD: Input-Output Whitened SVD for Adaptive-Rank LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15626)
*Ali Abbasi,Chayne Thrash,Haoran Qin,Hamed Pirsiavash,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IO-SVD，一种基于KL散度感知的双侧白化空间的SVD后训练压缩方法，通过异构秩分配和损失感知的混合SVD-量化策略，在保持模型性能的同时显著降低LLM/VLM的存储和计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在资源受限和延迟敏感场景下部署面临存储和计算成本高的瓶颈，现有SVD后训练压缩方法（如仅依赖输入白化空间、同构秩分配或损失不可知的分配启发式）在激进压缩下难以保持模型质量。

Method: (1) 提出输入-输出白化SVD（IO-SVD），构建KL感知的双侧白化空间（输出侧通过KL损失二阶展开捕捉预测敏感性，输入侧捕捉激活统计）；(2) 提出高效异构秩分配策略，使用一阶校准损失估计对白化奇异分量评分，在全局预算下剪枝最不敏感分量；(3) 提出损失感知重映射，基于量化导致的预测损失变化选择低秩因子行进行8-bit量化，改进混合SVD-量化压缩。

Result: 在多种LLM和VLM家族上的广泛实验以及推理时分析表明，IO-SVD在实现实际推理加速的同时，以最小性能下降压缩LLM。

Conclusion: IO-SVD通过KL感知的双侧白化、异构秩分配和损失感知混合量化，有效解决了现有SVD压缩方法的局限性，为资源受限场景下的大模型部署提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Large language models deliver strong performance across language and reasoning tasks, but their storage and compute costs remain major barriers to deployment in resource-constrained and latency-sensitive settings. SVD-based post-training compression offers a hardware-agnostic way to reduce model size and improve inference efficiency through low-rank factorization. However, existing methods often rely on input-only whitening spaces, homogeneous rank allocation, or loss-agnostic allocation heuristics, limiting their ability to preserve model quality under aggressive compression. We propose Input-Output Whitened SVD (IO-SVD), a post-training compression method that forms a KL-aware double-sided whitening space for model weights. Using a second-order expansion of the KL loss over the top-K token probabilities, IO-SVD constructs an output-side metric that captures predictive sensitivity, while input whitening captures activation statistics. We further introduce an efficient heterogeneous rank-allocation strategy that scores whitened singular components using first-order calibration loss estimates and prunes the least sensitive components under a global budget. Inspired by prior work that combines SVD truncation with quantization, we improve hybrid SVD-quantization compression through loss-aware remapping, which selects low-rank factor rows for 8-bit quantization based on the predicted loss change incurred by quantizing them. Extensive experiments across diverse LLM and VLM families, and inference-time analysis shows that IO-SVD compresses LLMs with minimal performance degradation while delivering practical inference speedups. Code is available at https://github.com/mint-vu/IO-SVD.git

</details>


### [111] [Sharp Spectral Thresholds for Logit Fixed Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15651)
*Tongxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了仿射softmax反馈系统的稳定性阈值应为β‖ΠWΠ‖_{𝒯→𝒯} < 2，而非经典理论中更保守的条件，从而扩展了系统可被证明稳定的参数范围，并精确指出了真正的相变点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典理论仅能保证系统在强随机化（过正则化）区域稳定，遗漏了介于稳定与不稳定之间的一个完整参数区间。现有条件无法识别系统发生质变的真实临界点，因此需要更精确的阈值分析。

Method: 针对有限维仿射logit系统，推导了维度无关的欧氏空间稳定阈值，并对比了经典条件与新条件的区别，通过理论分析填补了此前缺失的分岔前区域。

Result: 新的稳定阈值为β‖ΠWΠ‖_{𝒯→𝒯} < 2，该条件维度无关且比经典条件更宽松，能够将稳定保证扩展到奖励响应但全局可预测的系统。

Conclusion: 新阈值扩大了可证明稳定的边界，并准确识别了系统发生相变的真实位置，为熵正则化强化学习、logit博弈动力学等领域的稳定性分析提供了更精确的理论基础。

Abstract: Softmax feedback systems are a common mathematical core of entropy-regularized reinforcement learning, logit game dynamics, population choice, and mean-field variational updates. Their central stability question is simple: when does a self-reinforcing softmax system produce a unique and globally predictable outcome? Classical theory gives a conservative answer. By treating softmax as a unit-scale response, it certifies stability only in a strongly randomized regime.
  We prove that the classical approach misses an entire stable regime and does not identify the point at which the qualitative change truly occurs. For finite-dimensional affine logit systems, the sharp dimension-free Euclidean threshold is $$β\|ΠWΠ\|_{\mathcal T\to\mathcal T}<2,$$ rather than the previously used condition, which certifies stability only while the softmax system remains safely over-regularized. Our theorem fills the previously missing pre-bifurcation regime, extending stability guarantees for affine softmax feedback systems to reward-responsive yet globally predictable systems. It enlarges the certified stability boundary for these systems and identifies where the model genuinely undergoes a phase transition.

</details>


### [112] [On the Power of Adaptivity for $\varepsilon$-Best Arm Identification in Linear Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15663)
*Arnab Maiti,Yunbei Xu,Kevin Jamieson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了线性bandit中ε-最优臂识别的极小化样本复杂度。首先提出了一个非自适应固定设计方法，样本复杂度为O(d log(1/δ)/ε² + w(χ)²/ε²)，并证明了匹配的下界。然后探讨了自适应采样的优势，发现对于超立方体、ℓ₂球、m-集和多任务多臂bandit等结构化动作集，自适应仅能带来对数因子改进。最后首次构造了一个动作集，使得自适应能带来多项式因子的改进，关键在于一个ℓ₂范数估计子程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解线性bandit中ε-最优臂识别问题的极小化样本复杂度，特别是非自适应与自适应方法之间的能力差距，以及动作集结构对样本复杂度的影响。

Method: 1) 提出非自适应固定设计方法并推导其上界；2) 证明匹配的极小化下界；3) 分析超立方体、ℓ₂球、m-集、多任务多臂bandit等结构下自适应的对数因子改进；4) 构造一个动作集使得自适应获得多项式因子改进，关键组件是一个ℓ₂范数估计子程序。

Result: 非自适应固定设计方法的样本复杂度为Θ(d log(1/δ)/ε² + w(χ)²/ε²)；对于多种自然动作集自适应仅带来对数因子改进；首次构造了一个动作集使得自适应带来多项式因子改进。

Conclusion: 动作集的结构对自适应的增益有决定性影响：对于许多自然动作集自适应优势有限，但存在特定动作集可使自适应带来多项式级别的样本复杂度改进。

Abstract: We study the minimax sample complexity of $\varepsilon$-best arm identification in linear bandits. Given a compact action set $\mathcal{X}$ that spans $\mathbb{R}^d$ and an unknown reward vector $θ\in\mathbb{R}^d$, the goal is to output an arm $\widehat{x}\in\mathcal{X}$ such that $\langle \widehat{x},θ\rangle \ge \max_{x\in\mathcal{X}} \langle x,θ\rangle - \varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$, using as few samples as possible.
  First, we present a non-adaptive fixed-design method with sample complexity $\mathcal{O}\!\left(\frac{d\log(1/δ)}{\varepsilon^2}+\frac{w(\mathcal{X})^2}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$, where $w(\mathcal{X})$ is a Gaussian width term dependent on $\mathcal{X}$, and we prove a matching lower bound $Ω\!\left(\frac{d\log(1/δ)}{\varepsilon^2}+\frac{w(\mathcal{X})^2}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ for all non-adaptive fixed-design methods.
  We then turn to adaptive sampling. We raise an important structural question: beyond the canonical basis, are there structured action sets for which adaptivity yields only logarithmic-factor improvements over the optimal non-adaptive rate? We answer in the affirmative for several natural action sets, namely the hypercube, the $\ell_2$ ball, $m$-sets, and multi-task multi-armed bandits.
  Finally, we provide the first construction of an action set $\mathcal{X}$ for which adaptivity yields a polynomial-factor improvement over every non-adaptive algorithm. A key ingredient behind this separation is an $\ell_2$-norm estimation subroutine: we design an adaptive algorithm that uses $\mathcal{O}\!\left(\frac{d\log(1/δ)}{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ samples from the unit $\ell_2$ ball in $\mathbb{R}^d$ and outputs an estimate $\widehat r$ satisfying $|\widehat r-\|θ\|_2|\le \varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$, where $θ$ is the unknown reward vector.

</details>


### [113] [Interaction-Aware Influence Functions for Group Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15675)
*Jaeseung Heo,Kyeongheung Yun,Youngbin Choi,Sehyun Hwang,Jungseul Ok,Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种交互感知的影响函数，通过二阶泰勒展开捕捉训练样本间的相互作用对目标函数的影响，在留组估计和大模型指令微调数据选择中优于传统一阶影响函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准影响函数对一组样本的影响仅做简单求和，无法区分样本间的冗余性与互补性，即无法捕捉样本间的交互效应。

Method: 将目标函数在训练参数处展开至二阶，得到包含标准一阶项和两两交互项的估计器，其中交互项刻画了两个样本对目标影响的对齐程度。

Result: 在6个数据集-模型组合（逻辑回归、MLP、ResNet-9）上，该方法在所有设置中显著优于一阶影响函数；在Llama-3.1-8B指令微调数据选择中，在7个下游任务的5个上超越此前基于影响函数和表示相似性的基线方法。

Conclusion: 二阶交互项能有效捕捉样本间的协同与冗余作用，在群体影响估计和数据筛选任务中均带来显著提升，特别是在标准一阶影响函数失效（低于随机选择）的场景中仍表现优异。

Abstract: Influence functions approximate how removing a training example changes a quantity of interest, called the target function, such as a held-out loss. To estimate the influence of a group of examples, the standard practice is to sum the individual influences of its members. However, this sum does not capture how examples jointly affect the target: a pair of examples may be redundant or complementary, but the sum cannot distinguish these cases. We propose an interaction-aware influence function that characterizes how interactions between examples influence the target. By expanding the target to second order around the trained parameters, we obtain an estimator that augments the standard sum with a pairwise interaction term that captures the alignment between two examples' effects on the target. We empirically evaluate our estimator in two settings. First, on six dataset-model pairs spanning logistic regression, MLPs, and ResNet-9, our estimator tracks leave-group-out retraining substantially better than first-order influence across all settings. Second, when used as a greedy selection rule for instruction-tuning data on Llama-3.1-8B, it beats prior influence-based and representation-similarity baselines on five of seven downstream tasks, in a regime where standard influence-based selection underperforms random selection.

</details>


### [114] [SEED: Targeted Data Selection by Weighted Independent Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15691)
*Yuan Zhang,Lifeng Guo,Junwen Pan,Chang Liu,Wenzhao Zheng,Kuan Cheng,Kurt Keutzer,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SEED数据选择方法，将数据选择建模为加权独立集问题，通过节点价值校准和局部尺度归一化两个改进，在指令微调、视觉指令微调和语义分割任务上持续超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据选择方法在平衡样本质量与集合多样性时面临两个关键问题：(1) 朴素节点权重无法区分信息性信号与梯度噪声；(2) 异构领域分布下的边构建会产生结构失衡的图，导致选择偏向稀疏区域。

Method: 将数据选择建模为相似图上的加权独立集(WIS)问题：节点由影响力加权的数据样本表示，边连接语义冗余对。提出两个改进：(1) 节点价值校准——将影响力估计限制在双边显著子空间；(2) 局部尺度归一化——根据局部邻域密度自适应调整边阈值。

Result: 构建了Honeybee-Remake-SEED-200K紧凑多模态数据集，在指令微调、视觉指令微调和语义分割任务上 consistently 超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 从统一图视角出发的两个改进有效解决了数据选择中的关键挑战，SEED是一个鲁棒且可扩展的数据选择框架。

Abstract: Data selection seeks to identify a compact yet informative subset from large-scale training corpora, balancing sample quality against collection diversity. We formulate this problem as a Weighted Independent Set (WIS) on a similarity graph, where nodes represent data samples weighted by influence, and edges connect semantically redundant pairs. This formulation naturally yields subsets that are simultaneously high-quality and diverse. However, two challenges arise in practice: naive node weights fail to distinguish informative signals from gradient noise, and edge construction under heterogeneous domain distributions produces structurally imbalanced graphs that bias selection toward sparse regions. To address these issues, we introduce two principled refinements from a unified graph perspective: (1) \textit{node value calibration} that restricts influence estimation to the bilateral salient subspace to ground node importance in task-relevant signals rather than surface-level statistics; (2) \textit{local scale normalization} that adapts edge thresholds to local neighborhood density, mitigating graph imbalance induced by cross-domain distribution shifts. Together, these components yield a robust and scalable data selection pipeline dubbed SEED. We further construct \texttt{Honeybee-Remake-SEED-200K}, a compact multimodal dataset curated by SEED. Extensive experiments show that SEED consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods on instruction tuning, visual instruction tuning, and semantic segmentation across diverse model families.

</details>


### [115] [Tighter Regret Bounds for Contextual Action-Set Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15692)
*Zijun Chen,Zihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有固定奖励和转移函数但每幕允许动作集不同的情景强化学习问题，将MVP算法扩展到此框架，推导了对抗性和随机性上下文下的最小最大遗憾界，并给出了基于间隔的遗憾界改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实场景中（如个性化推荐、医疗方案选择），每轮可用的动作集（action set）可能因环境限制而变化（如物品库存变化、药物禁忌）。现有强化学习理论多假设动作集固定，无法直接应用于这类动态场景。本文旨在将具有上下文相关动作集的强化学习问题形式化，并建立理论保证。

Method: 将MVP（Model-based Value Prediction）算法扩展至允许每幕动作集不同的框架中。利用动作上下文M^k表示每幕可用动作集，并在算法设计中融入该信息。通过修改价值函数估计和探索策略，使其适应上下文变化。理论分析方面，针对对抗性上下文推导最小最大遗憾界，并导出基于间隔（gap-dependent）的改进上界。

Result: 1) 对抗性上下文下遗憾界为Õ(√(SAH³K log L))；2) 随机性上下文下遗憾界为Õ(√(SAH³K))；3) 固定上下文分布下的样本复杂度为Õ(SAH³/ε²)；4) 推导了基于间隔的遗憾界，当子最优间隙较大时可显著优于最小最大速率。

Conclusion: 本文成功将强化学习理论从固定动作集扩展到上下文相关动作集场景，证明了MVP算法在此框架下的最优性（在对抗性上下文中至多对数因子），同时间隔依赖界揭示了问题难度与子最优间隙的关系，为实际应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study episodic reinforcement learning with fixed reward and transition functions, but with episode-dependent admissible action sets that are observed at the start of each episode. Performance is measured by cumulative regret against the episode-wise optimal value, $\sum_{k=1}^K [V^{*,M^k} - V^{π^k,M^k}]$, where $M^k$ represents the action context in the $k$-th episode. We show that the MVP algorithm naturally extends to this framework and enjoys strong theoretical guarantees. In particular, we establish a minimax regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K\log L})$ for adversarial contexts, where $L$ denotes the number of possible contexts. This result implies a regret bound of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{SAH^3K})$ for stochastic contexts. We further translate the stochastic regret guarantee into a sample complexity bound of $\widetilde{O}(SAH^3/ε^2)$ for a fixed context distribution.
  In addition, we derive a gap-dependent regret bound of \[ \widetilde O\left( \inf_{p\in [0,1)} \left( \frac{1}{Δ_{\min}^{p}} + pKΔ_{\min}^{p} \right)\log K \cdot \mathrm{poly}(S,A,H) \right), \] where $Δ_{\min}^{p}$ is the global $p$-trimmed positive-gap floor over suboptimal $(h,s,a)$ triples. This bound can substantially improve upon the minimax rate when the relevant suboptimality gaps are large.

</details>


### [116] [Going Beyond the Edge: Distributed Inference of Transformer Models on Ultra-Low-Power Wireless Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15694)
*Alexander Gräfe,Ding Huo,Johannes Berger,Marco Zimmerling,Sebastian Trimpe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CATS是一个分布式Transformer推理框架，允许超低功耗无线设备通过协同推理执行比单设备大14倍的模型，采用通信感知的划分策略、SomeGather剪枝通信原语和消息丢失鲁棒训练技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型在IoT应用中需求激增，但其计算和内存需求远超单个超低功耗无线设备的能力，亟需一种分布式推理方案来突破单设备资源瓶颈。

Method: 提出了CATS框架，包含：(1) SomeGather——选择性广播激活列的剪枝通信原语；(2) 基于SomeGather的模型划分方法实现高效模型并行；(3) message-dropout训练策略模拟丢包以增强模型对无线通信不可靠性的鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实实验中，CATS首次实现了在多达16个超低功耗无线设备上的分布式Transformer推理，协同执行的模型规模可达单个设备的14倍。

Conclusion: CATS通过通信感知的协同设计（模型划分、无线通信和训练三方面联合优化），有效解决了资源受限设备上的大模型推理难题，为Transformer在IoT场景的部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Transformer models are rapidly becoming a cornerstone of modern Internet of Things (IoT) applications, yet their computational and memory demands far exceed the capabilities of a single typical ultra-low-power IoT device. We present CATS, a framework for distributed transformer inference on ultra-low-power wireless devices, enabling multiple devices to collaboratively execute models far larger than what a single device can sustain. At its core, CATS is a communication-aware distributed transformer inference scheme co-designed across transformer partitioning, wireless communication and training. It employs SomeGather, a new pruned communication primitive that selectively broadcasts activation columns to reduce communication bandwidth and RAM usage without sacrificing model accuracy. Building on SomeGather, we design a partitioning method that exploits this primitive for efficient model parallelism. To cope with unreliable wireless communication, CATS employs message-dropout during training, which mimics packet losses and yields models that are robust to message loss during inference. In real-world experiments, we show that CATS brings distributed transformer inference to ultra-low-power wireless devices for the first time, with deployments on up to 16 devices that collaboratively execute transformer models up to 14 times larger than what a single device can run.

</details>


### [117] [AGOP-IxG: A Gradient Covariance Filter for Local Feature Attribution on Tabular Data, with a Controlled Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15700)
*Raj Kiran Gupta Katakam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AGOP-IxG，一种基于平均梯度外积矩阵（AGOP）的快速逐样本特征归因方法，在三个合成表格数据集上相比SHAP、Integrated Gradients、InputXGradient和LIME，在Spearman秩相关、噪声特征质量上领先，速度比SHAP快350-1650倍；在真实数据集上的全局保真度评估中，各方法性能接近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动机器学习流程中产生的模型预测需要解释，但特征归因方法通常凭经验选择而非依据保真度评估，因为真实数据缺乏归因的真实标签。本文旨在提出一种快速且保真度可验证的归因方法。

Method: AGOP-IxG方法将每个样本的梯度预乘以一个top-K秩截断的平均梯度外积矩阵（Average Gradient Outer Product, AGOP）。在三个合成多分类表格任务（线性、稀疏非线性、基于交互）上，利用可解析或数值推导的真实归因标签，比较五种方法：AGOP-IxG、SHAP（DeepExplainer）、Integrated Gradients、InputXGradient和LIME。全局保真度评估在Adult Income和Credit Card Default数据集上使用ROAR协议进行。

Result: 在三个合成数据集上，AGOP-IxG在Spearman秩相关和噪声特征质量指标上均领先；在交互数据集上，top-k精度也领先。AGOP-IxG比SHAP快约350至1650倍。在真实数据集上的全局保真度评估中，各方法相对AUC差异在约1.7%以内，表明AGOP-IxG更针对逐样本局部归因而非全局特征排名。

Conclusion: AGOP-IxG是一种快速且保真度高的逐样本局部归因方法，在合成基准上优于现有主流方法，适合AutoML场景中的模型解释需求。

Abstract: Automated machine learning pipelines increasingly produce models whose predictions must be explained to end users, auditors, and downstream decision systems. The most widely used feature attribution methods (SHAP, Integrated Gradients, LIME) are typically chosen by convention rather than measured fidelity, because rigorous evaluation is impeded by the absence of ground-truth attribution on real data. We propose AGOP-IxG, a fast per-sample attribution method for tabular classifiers that pre-multiplies the per-sample gradient by a top-$K$ rank-truncated Average Gradient Outer Product matrix, and evaluate it against four widely-used baselines on a controlled tabular benchmark designed for AutoML practitioners. In Part 1, we construct three synthetic multi-class tabular tasks (linear, sparse nonlinear, interaction-based) where ground-truth attribution per sample is analytically or numerically derivable, and compare five methods: AGOP-IxG, SHAP (DeepExplainer), Integrated Gradients, InputXGradient, and LIME. AGOP-IxG leads on Spearman rank correlation and noise feature mass on all three synthetic datasets, and on top-$k$ precision on the interaction dataset. Across all settings, AGOP-IxG is approximately $350\times$ to $1{,}650\times$ faster than SHAP. In Part 2, we evaluate global faithfulness on Adult Income and Credit Card Default using the ROAR protocol; the methods cluster within $\sim 1.7\%$ relative AUC, consistent with AGOP-IxG being optimized for per-sample local attribution rather than global feature ranking.

</details>


### [118] [Differentiable Mixture-of-Agents Incentivizes Swarm Intelligence of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15706)
*Xingjian Wu,Junkai Lu,Siyu Yan,Xiangfei Qiu,Jilin Hu,Chenjuan Guo,Bin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可微混合智能体（DMoA）框架，通过可微的上下文感知路由机制和预测熵自监督信号，实现多智能体系统的动态推理路径选择与自适应协作，在9个基准测试中达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模语言模型驱动的多智能体系统通常依赖预定义或预编译的通信拓扑结构，缺乏应对动态任务需求的灵活性和自适应性。

Method: 设计可微的上下文感知路由机制，利用循环结构融合历史与上下文信息以逐步生成稀疏智能体激活；引入预测熵作为自监督信号优化路由过程，实现无需外部标注的测试时自适应。

Result: 在9个基准测试上达到最先进性能，同时展现出强效率、鲁棒性和集成能力。

Conclusion: DMoA通过动态路由与自监督优化克服了静态拓扑限制，为多智能体系统在复杂推理任务中的弹性协作提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have catalyzed the development of multi-agent systems (MAS) for complex reasoning tasks. However, existing MAS typically rely on pre-defined or pre-compiled communication topologies, which limits their flexibility and adaptability to dynamic task requirements. In this work, we propose Differentiable Mixture-of-Agents (DMoA), a self-evolving multi-agent framework that enables elastic and adaptive agent collaboration during inference. Instead of statically constructing workflows, DMoA dynamically routes and activates agents at each reasoning step, allowing the system to implicitly simulate diverse communication topologies and adapt to evolving demands. To achieve this, we design a differentiable, context-aware routing mechanism that leverages recurrent structures to incorporate historical and contextual information, producing sparse agent activations in a step-wise manner. Furthermore, we introduce predictive entropy as self-supervised signals to optimize the routing process, enabling efficient test-time adaptation without external annotations. Extensive experiments across 9 benchmarks demonstrate that DMoA achieves state-of-the-art performance while exhibiting strong efficiency, robustness, and ensembling capabilities.

</details>


### [119] [Bidirectional Fusion Guided by Cardiac Patterns for Semi-Supervised ECG Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15722)
*Jeonghwa Lim,Minje Park,Sunghoon Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CardioMix框架，一种基于双向CutMix策略的心电分割半监督学习方法，通过心电模式引导的数据混合增强标记与未标记数据集间的信息交换，在多种半监督算法和数据集上均取得优于现有融合策略的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心电波形精确分割对心血管诊断至关重要，但标注数据稀缺限制了深度学习模型训练。现有半监督语义分割方法主要关注未标记数据的一致性，未能充分利用标记集与未标记集之间的信息交换。

Method: 提出CardioMix框架，采用双向CutMix策略，由心电模式引导数据混合：一方面用未标记数据的真实变体丰富标记集，另一方面对未标记集施加更强的监督信号。心电模式引导确保所有增强样本保持生理意义。该框架设计为即插即用模块，兼容多种半监督分割算法。

Result: 在公开多数据集基准SemiSegECG上的大量实验表明，CardioMix作为即插即用模块，在多种数据集和不同标注比例下，一致优于现有的基于CutMix的融合策略。

Conclusion: CardioMix通过双向模式引导的CutMix策略有效促进了标记与未标记数据间的信息交换，为半监督心电分割提供了一种高效且兼容性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate delineation of electrocardiogram (ECG), the segmentation of meaningful waveform features, is crucial for cardiovascular diagnostics. However, the scarcity of annotated data poses a significant challenge for training deep learning models. Conventional semi-supervised semantic segmentation (SemiSeg) methods primarily focus on consistency from unlabeled data, underutilizing the information exchange possible between labeled and unlabeled sets. To address this, we introduce CardioMix, a framework built on a bidirectional CutMix strategy guided by cardiac patterns for ECG segmentation. This approach enriches the labeled set with realistic variations from unlabeled data while simultaneously applying stronger supervisory signals to the unlabeled set, as the cardiac pattern-guided mixing ensures all augmented samples remain physiologically meaningful. Our framework is designed as a plug-and-play module, demonstrating high compatibility with various SemiSeg algorithms. Extensive experiments on SemiSegECG, a public multi-dataset benchmark for ECG delineation, demonstrate that CardioMix consistently outperforms existing CutMix-based fusion strategies across diverse datasets and labeled ratios as a plug-and-play module compatible with various SemiSeg algorithms.

</details>


### [120] [GOMA: Toward Structure-Driven Multimodal Alignment from a Graph Signal Smoothing Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15723)
*Xu Wang,Xunkai Li,Yinlin Zhu,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GOMA框架，利用多模态属性图(MAG)的结构信息对冻结的视觉-语言嵌入进行后对齐优化，通过解耦消息流向、传播方式和平滑深度三个关键设计，在无监督图上下文中显著提升多模态检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CLIP风格的双编码器仅从孤立的图文对中学习模态对齐，未能利用实体间的关系上下文；直接在图结构上传播会导致模态间拓扑差异、过平滑等问题，亟需一种统一的结构驱动后对齐方案。

Method: 提出GOMA框架：(1)学习模态感知的传播算子；(2)执行有限步耦合平滑（禁止跨模态对角捷径）；(3)自适应读取节点特定平滑轨迹以在语义边界崩溃前保留有用信息。图仅作为无标注上下文，且移除自环边。

Result: 在7个MAG基准数据集上达到SOTA或并列SOTA的检索性能，且显著优于最强图基线方法的稳定性。

Conclusion: MAG结构可以作为一种有效的后编码器用于优化冻结的多模态嵌入，证明结构信息能弥补孤立图文对训练的不足。

Abstract: Multimodal alignment is commonly learned from isolated image-text pairs via CLIP-style dual encoders, leaving the relational context among entities largely unused. Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs), where nodes carry multimodal attributes and edges encode corpus structure, provide a natural setting for refining frozen vision-language embeddings. This refinement is challenging: visual, textual, and cross-modal relations often induce different neighborhood geometries, while unrestricted graph propagation can quickly over-smooth retrieval representations. Effectively leveraging graph context therefore requires simultaneously breaking modality-specific topological barriers, controlling the smoothing regime, and preserving informative smoothing before semantic boundaries collapse. We propose Graph-Optimized Multimodal Alignment (GOMA), a structure-driven post-alignment framework that views frozen multimodal embeddings as graph signals and addresses these requirements through a unified retrieval-oriented design. GOMA decouples three key design choices: where messages should flow, how multimodal evidence should propagate, and which smoothing depth should be retained. Concretely, it learns modality-aware propagation operators, performs finite-step coupled smoothing without diagonal cross-modal shortcuts, and adaptively reads out node-specific smoothing trajectories to preserve useful smoothing before collapse. All experiments follow a transductive MAG retrieval protocol where the graph serves only as unlabeled context and diagonal self-pair edges are removed. On seven MAG benchmarks, GOMA achieves state-of-the-art or tied state-of-the-art retrieval and remains substantially more stable than the strongest graph competitor, demonstrating that MAG structure can serve as an effective post-encoder for frozen multimodal embeddings.

</details>


### [121] [A Unified Perturbation Framework for Analyzing Leaderboard Stability and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15761)
*Hosna Oyarhoseini,Jimmy Lin,Amir-Hossein Karimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一扰动框架，证明LMArena等基于Bradley-Terry模型的排行榜在微小扰动下极不稳定（<1%扰动即可改变榜首），并利用影响函数高效实现定向操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型评估排行榜广泛应用但对其排序鲁棒性缺乏系统理解，需要量化扰动对排名稳定性的影响。

Method: 基于影响函数的统一扰动框架，研究三种匹配级扰动（Drop/Add/Flip）和玩家移除，通过top-k成员、Kendall's tau和置信区间不确定性三个指标评估影响。

Result: 在Chatbot Arena等7个数据集上，<1%的定向扰动即可改变榜首模型、降低Kendall's tau并改变置信区间；影响分数还能高效实现模型晋升/降级，减少不确定性所需的操作少于现有基线。

Conclusion: 当前排行榜缺乏鲁棒性，本文框架可作为审计工具，推动更稳健的评估协议设计。

Abstract: Evaluation leaderboards such as LMArena play a central role in benchmarking large language models by aggregating pairwise human preferences into model rankings, yet the robustness of these rankings remains poorly understood. We present a unified perturbation framework for analyzing Bradley-Terry leaderboards under structured data modifications using influence-based approximations. Our framework studies three match-level perturbations -- Drop, Add, and Flip -- together with player removal, and evaluates their effects on top-k membership, global ranking consistency via Kendall's tau, and confidence-interval-based uncertainty. Across Chatbot Arena and six additional pairwise-comparison datasets, we show that modern leaderboards are non-robust across all three objectives: sub-1% targeted perturbations can change the top-ranked model, degrade Kendall's tau, and alter confidence intervals. Beyond robustness auditing, we show that the same influence scores enable efficient targeted perturbations, promoting or demoting specific models and reducing target-model uncertainty with fewer actions than previous manipulation and active-sampling baselines. By summarizing these effects with normalized dataset-level robustness scores, our framework provides a practical and helpful tool for auditing leaderboard stability and motivating more robust evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [122] [Continual Learning of Domain-Invariant Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15775)
*Pascal Janetzky,Tobias Schlagenhauf,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种持续学习中的域不变表示学习方法，通过结合重放训练和序列不变性对齐，在多个领域上学习并保持不变结构，从而提升对未见过目标域的泛化能力。在视觉、医疗、制造和生态等六个数据集上，该方法优于现有持续学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法在多个领域顺序训练时，容易学习到领域特定的虚假线索（捷径学习），导致对未见过的目标域泛化能力受限。本文旨在通过持续学习域不变表示来解决这一局限性。

Method: 提出一类结合重放训练与序列不变性对齐的持续学习方法，顺序学习并保持跨域的不变结构（潜在因果机制），以减少对领域特定线索的过拟合。

Result: 在视觉、医学、制造业和生态学六个基准和真实世界数据集上，该方法在未见过目标域的泛化性能上持续优于现有持续学习基线；消融实验表明，简单扩展现有域不变表示学习方法到顺序训练中收益有限。

Conclusion: 本文首次为持续学习开发了域不变表示学习方法，证明通过保持跨域不变结构可以有效提升模型在部署时对未见过目标域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) aims to train models sequentially over multiple domains without forgetting previously learned knowledge. However, existing CL methods optimize for in-domain performance and are therefore prone to learning spurious, domain-specific cues (``shortcut learning''), which limits generalization to unseen domains after deployment. In this paper, we address this limitation through continual learning of domain-invariant representation. We introduce a broad class of CL methods that sequentially learn representations capturing invariant structures across domains. Our methods are motivated by the observation that such invariant structures often preserve the underlying causal mechanisms, which can reduce the risk of overfitting to domain-specific cues and thus offer better out-of-domain generalization. Our proposed CL methods combine replay-based training with a tailored sequential invariance alignment to learn -- and preserve -- invariant structures over time. We evaluate our methods under a deployment-oriented protocol that measures performance on unseen target domains. Across six benchmark and real-world datasets spanning vision, medicine, manufacturing, and ecology, our methods consistently outperform existing CL baselines in terms of generalization to unseen target domains. As an ablation, we further show that naïve extensions of sequential training with existing domain-invariant representation learning (DIRL) methods provide only limited benefits. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to develop domain-invariant representation methods for CL.

</details>


### [123] [AOT-POT: Adaptive Operator Transformation for Large-Scale PDE Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15793)
*Qitan Lv,Hong Wang,Zhongkai Hao,Wen Wu,Xuenan Xu,Bowen Zhou,Feng Wu,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AOT-POT方法，通过自适应的算子变换将复杂多样的PDE求解算子转化为统一、更易建模的形式，在仅增加3%参数的情况下，在12个PDE基准上实现SOTA性能，平均相对L2误差降低40.9%，微调后误差最高降低92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子预训练方法主要依赖增加模型容量来应对多PDE求解算子的复杂性和结构多样性，但忽略了目标算子本身的可优化性。受经典数值分析启发，本文旨在通过自适应输入依赖的算子变换，将复杂多样的求解算子转化为更简单、对齐更好的形式，从而降低多PDE联合建模的难度。

Method: AOT-POT在预训练算子Transformer中引入自适应算子变换机制：将隐藏表示扩展为多个并行流，在每个子层前后进行自适应聚合与再分配，并通过Sinkhorn投影的双随机矩阵实现流间混合，从而将多样化的求解算子重塑为统一形式，使单一架构能够有效建模整个算子族。

Result: 在12个PDE基准上，AOT-POT以仅3%的额外参数达到SOTA性能，相对L2误差最高降低77.6%，平均降低40.9%。微调后，域内PDE误差最高降低92%，域外PDE（预训练未见类型）误差最高降低89%。

Conclusion: 自适应算子变换是超越单纯扩展模型容量来推进PDE基础模型的有效且互补的方向。

Abstract: Pre-training neural operators on diverse partial differential equation (PDE) datasets has emerged as a promising direction for building general-purpose surrogate models in scientific machine learning. However, the inherent complexity and structural diversity of PDE solution operators make multi-PDE pre-training fundamentally challenging. Existing methods mainly address this by increasing model capacity, while leaving the target solution operators unchanged. Inspired by classical numerical analysis, we instead propose to transform complex and diverse solution operators into simpler, better-aligned forms that are easier to model jointly. Since the optimal transformation varies across PDE types, it must be adaptive and input-dependent, allowing a single neural operator to approximate an entire family of operators. We instantiate this idea as AOT-POT (adaptive operator-transformation for pre-training operator transformer), which expands hidden representations into multiple parallel streams, adaptively aggregates and redistributes them before and after each sub-layer, and mixes streams through Sinkhorn-projected doubly stochastic matrices for stable training. These mechanisms together reshape diverse solution operators into a unified form that can be effectively modeled by a single architecture. Empirically, AOT-POT achieves state-of-the-art performance on 12 PDE benchmarks with only 3\% additional parameters, reducing relative L2 error by up to 77.6\% (40.9\% on average). Fine-tuning AOT-POT further reduces L2 error by up to 92\% on in-domain PDEs and 89\% on out-of-domain PDEs (unseen types during pre-training), demonstrating that adaptive operator transformation is an effective and complementary direction for advancing PDE foundation models beyond simply scaling model capacity.

</details>


### [124] [Practical Validity Conditions for Byzantine-Tolerant Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15887)
*Mélanie Cambus,Darya Melnyk,Tijana Milentijević,Stefan Schmid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出最小包围球（MEB）有效性条件及其松弛变体 c-MEB 有效性，用于拜占庭容错联邦学习中的鲁棒聚合。严格 MEB 有效性受限于维度相关韧性，而松弛 c-MEB 有效性在多数客户端诚实（n>2t）时可实现。文中给出了最优 MinMax-MEB 聚合规则（c<√2），为多种标准聚合器提供了显式松弛 MEB 保证，并将 MEB 有效性与凸有效性、松弛凸有效性及 box 有效性系统关联，为拜占庭鲁棒聚合提供了几何有效性条件的系统映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸有效性条件虽然理论强大，但其维度相关的韧性和对许多实际聚合规则的排斥使其不适用于现代联邦学习系统，因此需要更灵活有效的几何有效性条件。

Method: 提出最小包围球（MEB）有效性条件及其乘性松弛 c-MEB 有效性，证明其可实现条件（n>2t），设计最优 MinMax-MEB 聚合规则（c<√2），推导标准聚合器（最小直径平均、medoid、几何中位数）的松弛 MEB 保证，并与现有有效性条件建立系统映射。

Result: 严格 MEB 有效性仍受限于有限韧性，而松弛 c-MEB 有效性在多数诚实条件下可实现；MinMax-MEB 达到最优界 c<√2；标准聚合器均满足显式松弛 MEB 保证；建立了 MEB 有效性与凸、松弛凸、box 有效性的统一框架。

Conclusion: 松弛 MEB 有效性连接了分布式计算中的有效性条件与拜占庭容错聚合规则，为凸有效性提供了实用的替代方案。

Abstract: Robust aggregation is the core operation in Byzantine-tolerant federated learning. To ensure the quality of aggregation independently of data distribution or attacks, validity conditions are needed. They provide geometric guarantees of where the output of the aggregation must lie. The widespread convex validity requires the output to lie in the convex hull of the honest vectors. Although this guarantee is strong in theory, it is poorly suited to modern federated learning systems, as it has dimension-dependent resilience and excludes many practical aggregation rules.
  We introduce the minimum enclosing ball (MEB) validity condition for robust aggregation, as well as its multiplicative relaxation, $c$-MEB validity, where $c$ is a constant. We show that exact MEB validity still suffers from limited resilience, while relaxed $c$-MEB validity is achievable if a majority of clients is honest, i.e. $n>2t$. We give an optimal MinMax-MEB rule for the relaxed condition with the bound $c<\sqrt{2}$ and prove explicit relaxed-MEB guarantees for standard aggregators including minimum-diameter averaging, medoid and geometric median. Finally, we relate MEB validity to convex, relaxed-convex and box validity studied in prior literature, thus providing a systematic map of geometric validity conditions for Byzantine-robust aggregation. Our results show that relaxed MEB validity connects validity conditions in distributed computing and Byzantine-tolerant aggregation rules, and offers a practical alternative to convex validity.

</details>


### [125] [Context-aware Entity-Relation Extraction for Threat Intelligence Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15904)
*Inoussa Mouiche,sherif Saad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CTiKG框架，通过融合SecureBERT+上下文嵌入和领域本体知识，构建混合NLP流水线用于从非结构化CTI报告中准确提取威胁实体及其关系，在DNRTI-AUG-STIX2数据集上相比基线实现NER提升3-4%、RE提升最高8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流水线的CKG构建方法受限于非结构化CTI报告的复杂结构、领域特定语言和语义歧义，导致错误传播、提取精度低且泛化能力差。

Method: 提出CTiKG框架，采用混合NLP模型融合SecureBERT+上下文嵌入与领域本体专家知识，构建流水线架构进行威胁实体识别与关系抽取。

Result: 在DNRTI-AUG-STIX2数据集上，NER性能提升3-4%，RE性能提升最高8%（基于精确率、召回率和F1分数）；在DNRTI和STUCCO基准上验证了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CTiKG框架能有效缓解错误传播问题，提升CTI实体关系抽取的准确性和泛化能力，且开源数据集可促进可重复性研究。

Abstract: Cybersecurity Knowledge Graphs (CKGs) unify diverse Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) sources into structured, queryable formats, offering scalable solutions for automating proactive and real-time security responses. Their increasing adoption has significantly enhanced the workflow and decision-making efficiency of security professionals. However, constructing CKGs requires extracting entity-relation triples from unstructured CTI reports, a task hindered by complex report structure, domain-specific language, and semantic ambiguity. As a result, existing pipeline-based approaches often suffer from error propagation, reducing extraction accuracy and limiting generalizability. This paper introduces the Context-aware Threat Intelligence Knowledge Graph (CTiKG) framework, a pipeline architecture designed to accurately extract and classify threat entities and their relationships from CTI reports. CTiKG incorporates hybrid NLP models that leverage SecureBERT+ contextual embeddings and expert knowledge from a domain ontology to reduce misclassifications and mitigate cascading errors. Experiments on the DNRTI-AUG-STIX2 dataset, which comprises 21 entity types aligned with STIX 2.1, demonstrate significant improvements over state-of-the-art baselines, yielding 3-4% gains in NER and up to 8% in RE performance, based on precision, recall, and F1-score. Additional validation on DNRTI and STUCCO benchmarks confirms the framework's robustness and practical applicability. All datasets, including the curated DNRTI-AUG-STIX2, are released on GitHub to foster reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [126] [LoCO: Low-rank Compositional Rotation Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15916)
*An Nguyen,Jaesik Choi,Anh Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoCO是一种通过低秩斜对称矩阵和组合旋转链构造正交变换的参数高效微调方法，在扩散Transformer、视觉Transformer和语言模型上表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法（如LoRA）虽能实现参数效率，但无法保持预训练表示的几何结构，限制了模型性能。

Method: 利用低秩斜对称矩阵构造正交变换，通过组合旋转链实现正交性，并提出近似方案实现组合旋转的完全并行计算，在保持低计算复杂度的同时控制近似误差。

Result: 在扩散Transformer、视觉Transformer适配和语言模型适配等多个领域验证，相比现有正交和非正交方法均表现出优越或具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: LoCO在保持参数效率的同时有效保留了预训练表示的几何结构，是一种实用且高效的PEFT方法。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has emerged as an critical technique for adapting large-scale foundation models across natural language processing and computer vision. While existing methods such as low-rank adaptations achieve parameter efficiency via low-rank weight updates, they are limited in their ability to preserve the geometric structure of pretrained representations. We introduce Low-rank Compositional Orthogonal fine-tuning (LoCO), a novel PEFT method that constructs orthogonal transformations through low-rank skew-symmetric matrices and compositional rotation chains. We propose an approximation scheme that enables fully parallel computation of compositional rotations, making the approach practical for high-dimensional feature spaces. Our method maintains low computational complexity while maintaining orthogonality with controlled approximation error. We validate LoCO across diverse domains, including diffusion transformer fine-tuning, vision transformer adaptation, and language model adaptation. Our method demonstrates superior or competitive performance compared to both existing orthogonal and non-orthogonal methods.

</details>


### [127] [When and Why Adversarial Training Improves PINNs: A Neural Tangent Kernel Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15959)
*Yuan-dong Cao,Chi Chiu SO,Jun-Min Wang,He Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的对抗训练PINNs分析框架，从理论上解释了GANs鉴别器如何影响PINNs训练动态，并开发出更高效实用的训练算法，精度提升达数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs在训练中面临谱偏差、刚性问题及高频/多尺度解精度差等难题，虽对抗训练有强经验效果但其内在机制尚不明确，缺乏理论解释。

Method: 首先建立理论框架解释对抗训练在PINNs中有效的条件与原因，然后统一分析各类GANs变体在PINNs训练中的作用，最终提出新的高效实用训练算法。

Result: 新方法显著缓解了PINNs训练的病理问题，模型性能优于现有方法，精度常提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 对抗训练通过GANs鉴别器影响PINNs训练动态具有理论依据，所提算法在实践中高效且准确。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) are powerful surrogates for differential equations but are notoriously difficult to train due to spectral bias, stiffness, and poor accuracy on high-frequency or multiscale solutions. Adversarial training based on generative adversarial networks (GANs) has recently gained surprisingly strong empirical results in improving training, but the underlying mechanisms remain elusive. To this end, we propose a new analysis framework for adversarially trained PINNs, based on the key observation of how the discriminator in GANs can influence the training dynamics of PINNs. The framework first provides a much needed theoretical grounding to why and when adversarial training is effective in PINNs, then presents a unified analysis of GANs variants in such training, and finally leads to a new, practical, efficient training algorithm for PINNs. Empirical results demonstrate that our method can significantly reduce the pathology of PINNs training, thereby providing better models with superior performances, often several magnitudes more accurate than alternative methods.

</details>


### [128] [Entropy-Based Characterisation of the Polarised Regime in Latent Variable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15965)
*Peter Clapham,Lisa Bonheme,Marek Grzes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于信息论的VAE极化态分类方法，通过均值表示的熵来识别活跃/被动/混合隐变量，并揭示方差信息对区分的必要性。实验证明被动维度在适当归一化后仍可提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAE极化态中活跃维度的识别标准依赖于高斯先验，限制了其在变分模型及其他先验下的适用性，需要更通用的分类方法。

Method: 基于均值表示熵的信息论分类；通过熵-方差界理论建立与KL最小化的联系；将所得准则与Bonheme的活跃/被动条件进行关联分析。

Result: 在β-VAE、可识别VAE、最小体积自编码器、L2正则化自编码器上一致恢复极化态；被动维度在合适的归一化下可对下游任务产生微小但一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 熵准则有效识别极化态但无法单独区分活跃与混合维度；隐变量坍缩更多是尺度问题而非信息完全移除。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) often exhibit a polarised regime in which latent variables separate into active, passive, and mixed subsets. Existing criteria for identifying active dimensions depend on a Gaussian prior, limiting their applicability to variational models and specific priors. We propose a simple information-theoretic classification of the polarised regime based on the entropy of the mean representation. We show theoretically how this entropy couples to KL minimisation through entropy--variance bounds, and we relate the resulting criterion to Bonheme's active/passive conditions. We also clarify a key limitation: entropy of the mean alone cannot reliably distinguish active from mixed dimensions without additional signals from the variance representation. Empirically, we evaluate the entropy criterion on $β$-VAEs, identifiable VAEs, Least-Volume Autoencoders, and L2-regularised autoencoders, and find that it consistently recovers a polarised regime when such a regime is present across the model classes studied. Finally, we show that passive dimensions can yield small but consistent improvements on downstream tasks when latent codes are appropriately normalised, suggesting that collapse is often a matter of scale rather than absolute information removal.

</details>


### [129] [Accelerated Gradient Descent for Faster Convergence with Minimal Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16017)
*Manuel Graca,L. Miguel Silveira,Arlindo Oliveira,Frank Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CT-AGD是一种针对深度学习中非凸优化问题的加速方法，通过有限差分捕捉局部曲率来加速一阶方法，并设计了启发式策略来减轻随机小批量训练中的噪声和偏差。在保持与Adam等自适应方法相当的存储和计算开销下，CT-AGD在达到相同精度水平时平均减少33%的训练轮数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习中非凸优化问题的训练效率有待提升，现有的一阶方法收敛速度较慢，需要大量训练轮数才能达到满意精度。

Method: CT-AGD通过有限差分商显式捕捉局部曲率信息来加速一阶优化方法，并设计了启发式策略以减轻随机小批量训练引入的噪声和偏差。

Result: 在达到与基线一阶方法相同精度水平的前提下，CT-AGD平均减少了33%的训练轮数。

Conclusion: CT-AGD是一种高效的优化方法，能够在保持精度不降低的同时显著加速深度学习模型的训练过程，且计算和存储开销与Adam等自适应方法相当。

Abstract: In this paper, we present CT-AGD (Curvature-Tuned Accelerated Gradient Descent), an optimization method for non-convex optimization problems in deep learning training tasks. CT-AGD is a general boosting procedure that accelerates first-order methods by explicitly capturing the local curvature using finite-difference quotients, and the development of heuristics aimed at mitigating noise and bias introduced by stochastic mini-batch training. CT-AGD has a comparable storage and computational overhead as adaptive gradient methods such as Adam. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that CT-AGD achieves the same level of accuracy as the baseline first-order methods, yet reduces the required training epochs by 33% on average.

</details>


### [130] [Ada-Diffuser: Latent-Aware Adaptive Diffusion for Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16054)
*Fan Feng,Selena Ge,Minghao Fu,Zijian Li,Yujia Zheng,Zeyu Tang,Yingyao Hu,Biwei Huang,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Ada-Diffuser框架，通过将潜在动态推断显式融入生成式决策过程，在最小充分观测条件下识别潜在过程，并利用因果扩散模型同时学习观测时间结构和潜在动态，实现自适应规划与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的序列建模决策方法常忽略具有演化动态的潜在因素（如环境转移、奖励结构、高层智能体行为），而这些因素对精准动力学建模和有效决策至关重要，因此需要显式建模隐藏过程。

Method: 提出Ada-Diffuser框架：在温和条件下从短时间观测块中识别潜在过程的理论保证；采用因果扩散模型同时学习观测交互的时间结构和底层潜在动态；模块化设计支持规划与策略学习任务。

Result: 在模拟控制和机器人基准实验上，Ada-Diffuser在准确潜在推断和自适应策略学习方面表现出有效性。

Conclusion: 显式建模潜在动态推理能有效提升生成式决策模型的泛化能力和适应性，Ada-Diffuser为潜在变量建模与决策学习的统一提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent work has framed decision-making as a sequence modeling problem using generative models such as diffusion models. Although promising, these approaches often overlook latent factors that exhibit evolving dynamics, elements that are fundamental to environment transitions, reward structures, and high-level agent behavior. Explicitly modeling these hidden processes is essential for both precise dynamics modeling and effective decision-making. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that explicitly incorporates latent dynamic inference into generative decision-making from minimal yet sufficient observations. We theoretically show that under mild conditions, the latent process can be identified from small temporal blocks of observations. Building on this insight, we introduce Ada-Diffuser, a causal diffusion model that learns the temporal structure of observed interactions and the underlying latent dynamics simultaneously, and furthermore, leverages them for planning and control. With a modular design, Ada-Diffuser supports both planning and policy learning tasks, enabling adaptation to latent variations in dynamics, rewards, and latent actions. Experiments on simulated control and robotic benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness in accurate latent inference and adaptive policy learning.

</details>


### [131] [SAFE Quantum Machine Learning with Variational Quantum Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16067)
*Ying Chen,Paolo Giudici,Vasily Kolesnikov,Paolo Recchia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过幅值编码和高维深度表示结合的变分量子分类器，利用可学习的经典预编码层和SAFE-AI指标评估，在精度、鲁棒性和可解释性上取得平衡表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子机器学习在安全关键场景下的可靠性，解决经典模型在鲁棒性和可解释性方面的不足。

Method: 采用幅值编码将经典深度表示映射到量子态空间，结合归一化幅值嵌入与有界量子可观测量构建平滑假设类；引入可学习经典预编码层稳定量子模型；使用Cramer von Mises散度导出的SAFE-AI指标进行多维度可靠性评估。

Result: 与经典基线相比，量子模型在预测性能上具有竞争力，同时在SAFE可靠性剖面中表现更均衡，对噪声鲁棒性更强，在结构化特征移除下稳定性更优。

Conclusion: 变分量子电路为安全关键场景下的稳定性导向SAFE学习提供了一种原理性机制。

Abstract: We propose a variational quantum classifier operating on high dimensional deep representations via amplitude encoding, stabilized by a learnable classical pre encoding layer.By combining normalized amplitude embeddings with bounded quantum observables, the resulting model induces a structured and smooth hypothesis class with controlled sensitivity to input variations. Model reliability is assessed using SAFE-AI metrics derived from the Cramer von Mises divergence, enabling consistent evaluation across accuracy, robustness, and explainability dimensions. Empirical results show that the proposed quantum model provides competitive predictive performance compared with strong classical baselines while exhibiting a more balanced SAFE reliability profile, with improved robustness to noise and stability under structured feature removal. These findings suggest that variational quantum circuits offer a principled mechanism for stability oriented SAFE learning in safety critical settings.

</details>


### [132] [ITGPT: Generative Pretraining on Irregular Timeseries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16069)
*Antoine Honoré,Ming Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ITGPT是一种基于注意力的架构，通过自监督学习和生成式预训练处理多模态、不规则采样的时间序列数据，无需重采样、特征融合或显式数据插补，在医疗和预测性维护任务上达到SOTA性能，且在标签稀缺时有效利用无标签数据优于纯监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列回归模型难以有效利用大规模多模态标记数据，尤其当数据存在不规则采样或缺失值时。现有Transformer模型虽在文本等结构化数据上表现优异，但缺乏处理不规则采样多模态时间序列的灵活性。

Method: 提出ITGPT架构，基于注意力机制，支持同时使用SSL损失和GPT风格目标进行训练，无需重采样、特征融合或显式数据插补即可处理多模态不规则采样时间序列。

Result: 在TIHM医疗数据集和CompX预测性维护数据集上达到SOTA性能；在标签稀缺场景下，通过SSL和GPT训练有效利用无标签数据，优于纯监督方法。

Conclusion: ITGPT为高效利用大规模非结构化时间序列数据用于实际推理任务迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Timeseries regression models often struggle to leverage large volumes of labeled multimodal data, particularly when the data are irregularly sampled or contain missing values. This is common in domains like healthcare and predictive maintenance, where data are collected from unreliable sources, and labeling requires expert knowledge or costly equipments. Transformer-based large language models have proven effective on structured data such as text through self-supervised learning (SSL) and generative pretraining (GPT) frameworks. However, such models lack the flexibility to efficiently process irregularly sampled multimodal timeseries data. In this paper, we introduce ITGPT, an attention-based architecture designed for handling multimodal, irregularly sampled timeseries by allowing training with both SSL losses and GPT-like objectives. We evaluate its performance on a healthcare task with the TIHM dataset, and a predictive maintenance task with the CompX dataset. Our results demonstrate that ITGPT achieves state-of-the-art performance without requiring resampling, feature fusion or explicit data imputation. Furthermore, when labels are scarce, ITGPT effectively leverages unlabeled data through SSL and GPT training, outperforming the purely supervised approach. This represents an important step towards efficiently using large and unstructured timeseries datasets for practical inference tasks.

</details>


### [133] [MIND: Decoupling Model-Induced Label Noise via Latent Manifold Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16081)
*Dayong Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模型诱导噪声解耦框架MIND，通过潜变量流形解耦将高维噪声分解为可处理的子空间依赖分量，有效应对预训练模型标注中的系统性噪声，在3D数据集和视觉语言模型幻觉校正中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练专家和基础模型驱动的自动标注范式引入了模型诱导的标签噪声，这种噪声与随机噪声不同，它源自标注者的归纳偏差，表现为与局部特征流形紧密耦合的系统性错误。现有基于全局转移矩阵的方法无法拟合这类结构性模式，而学习实例级转移矩阵在数学上难以处理。

Method: 提出模型诱导噪声解耦(MIND)框架，核心包括：1) 潜变量流形解耦理论，证明高维噪声流形可分解为子空间依赖的易处理分量；2) 潜变量解耦估计器(LDE)，动态将样本投影到具有一致错误模式的潜结构聚类中，无需真实锚点即可实现噪声可辨识性。

Result: 在CIFAR-100受控噪声实验、S3DIS和ScanNet等大规模3D数据集的结构性压力测试中，MIND显著优于现有最先进方法。同时有效校正了视觉语言模型(如OpenSeg)的零样本幻觉。

Conclusion: MIND为基础模型的鲁棒蒸馏提供了一个有潜力的框架，能够有效处理模型诱导的系统性噪声，并在多种复杂基准上展现出优越性能。

Abstract: The paradigm of learning from automatic annotations driven by pre-trained experts and Foundation Models dominates data-hungry applications. However, it introduces a critical challenge: model-induced label noise. Unlike stochastic noise in classical robust learning, this noise stems from annotator inductive biases, manifesting as systematic errors tightly coupled with local feature manifolds. Existing methods relying on global transition matrices underfit these structural patterns, while learning instance-specific matrices remains mathematically intractable. We propose Model-Induced Noise Decoupling (MIND), a theoretically grounded framework addressing this dilemma. We demonstrate that the high-dimensional noise manifold can be decoupled into tractable, subspace-dependent components via Latent Manifold Disentanglement. Specifically, our Latent Decoupling Estimator (LDE) dynamically projects samples into latent structural clusters with consistent error modes, facilitating noise identifiability without ground-truth anchor points. To rigorously evaluate robustness, we adopt a hierarchical protocol: moving from controlled noise on CIFAR-100 to a structural stress test on large-scale real-world 3D datasets (S3DIS, ScanNet), where error patterns explicitly couple with geometric manifolds. Empirically, MIND significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods on these complex benchmarks and effectively corrects zero-shot hallucinations from Vision-Language Models (e.g., OpenSeg), highlighting its potential as a robust distillation framework for Foundation Models.

</details>


### [134] [Multi-level Self-supervised Pretraining on Compositional Hierarchical Graph for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16088)
*Xiayu Liu,Zhengyi Lu,Hou-biao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MolCHG是一个基于组合层次化图的多级自监督预训练框架，通过原子图与键图并行架构、四类节点三级语义组织，以及三个层级特定的预训练任务，在MoleculeNet九大数据集上取得了七项最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子图自监督预训练方法大多在单一结构粒度上操作，将键信息仅作为辅助边属性处理，而非独立的语义层次，限制了模型对分子多层级结构信息的捕获能力。

Method: 提出组合层次化图（Compositional Hierarchical Graph），将分子结构组织为跨越三个语义级别的四类节点；引入与原子图并行的键图，使键级信息独立演化为节点表示；设计三个层级特定预训练目标：原子-键跨视图对比任务、片段级官能团预测任务、图级结构预测任务。

Result: 在MoleculeNet九大数据集的分类与回归任务中，MolCHG在七个数据集上取得最佳性能，在其余数据集上与最强基线保持竞争力；消融实验证实多级监督信号互补且各组件均有贡献。

Conclusion: 将键信息提升为独立语义层并结合多级自监督预训练，能有效提升分子性质预测性能，多级监督信号的互补性是模型成功的关键。

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining on molecular graphs has emerged as a promising approach for molecular property prediction, yet most existing methods operate at a single structural granularity and treat bond information as auxiliary edge attributes rather than as an independent semantic layer. In this work, we propose MolCHG, a multi-level self-supervised pretraining framework built upon a novel Compositional Hierarchical Graph that organizes molecular structure into four types of nodes across three semantic levels. By introducing a bond graph that operates in parallel with the atom graph, our architecture elevates bond-level information to independently evolving node representations, enabling fragment nodes to aggregate atom-level and bond-level semantics on an equal footing. We design three level-specific pretraining objectives: an atom-bond cross-view contrastive task that aligns the atom-view and bond-view representations within each fragment, a fragment-level functional group prediction task to inject domain-relevant chemical knowledge, and graph-level structure prediction tasks to encode global molecular topology. Experiments on nine MoleculeNet benchmarks demonstrate that MolCHG achieves the best performance on seven datasets across both classification and regression tasks, remaining competitive with the strongest baselines on the rest. Ablation studies further confirm that the multi-level supervision signals are complementary and that each component contributes to the overall performance.

</details>


### [135] [Centralized vs Decentralized Federated Learning: A trade-off performance analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16089)
*Chaimaa Medjadji,Guilain Leduc,Sylvain Kubler,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过Fedstellar模拟器、MNIST数据集和MLP分类器，对集中式、去中心化和半去中心化三种联邦学习架构进行了实验比较分析，填补了现有研究缺乏三种架构系统性对比的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对集中式(CFL)、去中心化(DFL)和半去中心化(SDFL)三种联邦学习架构进行系统性实验比较的工作，无法清晰理解各自的优势、局限及性能指标间的权衡关系。

Method: 使用Fedstellar模拟器，在MNIST数据集上采用MLP分类器，对CFL、DFL和SDFL三种FL架构进行实验对比分析。

Result: 通过实验比较揭示了三种FL架构各自的优势和局限性，以及不同性能指标之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 选择正确的FL架构需要根据应用需求权衡性能指标，本文的实验分析为架构选择提供了依据。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for collaborative model training across distributed edge devices while preserving data privacy especially with the huge increase amount of data due to the adoption of technologies which contributes to the growing number of IoT devices. Storing this amount of data centrally is challenging due to issues like limited communication, privacy, and regulations. FL can be Centralized (CFL), Decentralized (DFL), and Semi-decentralized (SDFL). Choosing the right FL architecture depends on the application's needs. However, very few research studies have experimentally compared these three types of architectures to not only understand the respective strengths and limitations, but also trade-offs between different performance indicators. This paper overcome this lack of analysis, conducting experimental analyses using the Fedstellar simulator, MNIST dataset, and MLP classifier.

</details>


### [136] [Federated Imputation under Heterogeneous Feature Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16099)
*Imane Hocine,Chaimaa Medjadji,Sylvain Kubler,Gregoire Danoy,Yves Le Traon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedHF-Impute框架，通过共享全局特征图的消息传递机制，解决联邦学习中客户端特征模式部分重叠时的数据补齐问题，在SECOM和AirQuality数据集上分别提升26.9%和8.4%的插补准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习方法假设客户端特征模式对齐，但在表格数据场景中客户端通常只观察到部分重叠的特征子集，导致参数平均方法（如FedAvg）在特征重叠弱或无重叠时信息传递有限，无法有效进行联邦插补。

Method: 将结构性特征不可用性与传统缺失值区分开，构建共享的全局特征图，通过消息传递在统计相关的特征间传播信息，实现间接的跨客户端知识迁移，同时保持标准联邦通信协议。

Result: 在SECOM数据集上比FL基线提升26.9%，在AirQuality数据集上提升8.4%，在PhysioNET上与最优基线仅差0.3%。

Conclusion: FedHF-Impute通过特征图消息传递有效解决了异构特征空间下的联邦插补问题，在部分模式重叠场景下显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative training across decentralized clients, but most methods assume aligned feature schemas, an assumption that rarely holds in tabular settings where clients observe only partially overlapping feature subsets. In these heterogeneous feature spaces, parameter-averaging methods (e.g., FedAvg) transfer little information across weakly overlapping or disjoint feature groups, limiting their effectiveness for federated imputation. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{FedHF-Impute}, a federated imputation framework that separates structural feature unavailability from conventional missingness and uses a shared global feature graph to propagate information across statistically related features through message passing. This enables indirect cross-client knowledge transfer, even when features are never jointly observed locally, while preserving standard federated communication. Under simulated partial schema overlap on the SECOM and AirQuality datasets, FedHF-Impute improves imputation accuracy (RMSE) over FL baselines by 26.9\%, and 8.4\% respectively, while achieving comparable performance on PhysioNET, with only a 0.3\% difference relative to the best baseline.

</details>


### [137] [Attention Dispersion in Dynamic Graph Transformers: Diagnosis and a Transferable Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16112)
*Jinhao Zhang,Kangfei Zhao,Qiuhao Zeng,Long-Kai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现动态图Transformer在时间分布偏移下存在注意力分散问题，提出用差分注意力替代标准注意力机制，并基于此构建DiffDyG模型，在9个基准上达到SOTA性能，尤其在高偏移数据集上提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的连续时间动态图学习方法在时间分布偏移场景下性能受限，作者识别出注意力分散（attention dispersion）是核心失效模式，即使关键节点存在于输入中，模型也无法聚焦于它们。

Method: 通过消融实验对比结构/时间上区分的历史邻居与随机邻居，证明存在携带更强预测信号的关键节点；提出用差分注意力（differential attention）替代标准注意力，抑制共模注意力并放大区分性token级信号；将差分注意力集成到三个代表性CTDG Transformer基线中，并构建DiffDyG参考实现。

Result: 在9个基准和三种负采样协议下，DiffDyG达到SOTA性能；高偏移数据集上性能增益尤其显著；注意力测量显示注意力熵降低、关键节点上的注意力质量增加。

Conclusion: 差分注意力是一种简单且可迁移的修复方案，能有效缓解动态图Transformer在时间分布偏移下的注意力分散问题，提升模型鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Transformer-based architectures have become the dominant paradigm for Continuous-Time Dynamic Graph (CTDG) learning, yet their performance remains limited on temporally shifted datasets. In this work, we identify attention dispersion as a shared failure mode of dynamic graph Transformers under temporal distribution shift. Through controlled ablation contrasting structurally and temporally distinguished historical neighbors against random ones, we show that prediction depends on a class of critical nodes that carry consistently more predictive signal than arbitrary neighbors. However, existing Transformers fail to focus on these nodes even when they are present in the input, as temporal shift weakens attention contrast and produces overly dispersed attention distributions. This diagnosis suggests a simple and transferable fix: replace standard attention with differential attention, which suppresses common-mode attention and amplifies distinctive token-level signals. When added to three representative CTDG Transformer baselines, differential attention consistently improves performance, with gains concentrated on high-shift datasets. Attention-level measurements further confirm the mechanism, showing reduced attention entropy and increased attention mass on critical nodes. Building on these findings, we introduce DiffDyG, a reference implementation combining differential attention with standard input encodings. Across 9 benchmarks and three negative sampling protocols, DiffDyG achieves SOTA performance, with especially large gains on the most shifted datasets.

</details>


### [138] [Entropy Across the Bridge: Conditional-Marginal Discretization for Flow and Schrödinger Samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16126)
*Bruno Trentini,Dejan Stancevic,Michael M. Bronstein,Alexander Tong,Luca Ambrogioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件-边际熵率的推理时间调度器，用于流匹配和薛定谔桥模型，在低函数评估预算下通过非均匀时间离散化显著提升采样质量，在2D桥模型、EDM/CIFAR-10和AlphaFlow蛋白质生成任务上均优于线性与余弦调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定小推理预算下，流式生成模型的样本质量高度依赖于采样器将有限函数评估分配在何处。现有的推理网格通常是启发式的或从一端扩散中继承而来，缺乏对桥几何结构的感知。

Method: 推导了一个条件-边际熵率目标（conditional-marginal entropy-rate objective）用于桥感知离散化，将端点条件桥几何与边际流演化分离；基于该目标构建了无训练的推理时间调度器；对于高斯布朗桥，该熵率具有闭式解且呈U形分布，从而激励边界加重的非均匀网格。

Result: 在2D桥/流模型上，10步ODE-Heun MMD较线性调度降低18.1%，SDE-Heun改进22.7%。在EDM/CIFAR-10上，5步FID达到186.3±4.0（线性200.5±2.9，余弦238.0±5.3）。在AlphaFlow蛋白质生成上，低NFE条件下在CAMEO22和ATLAS基准上均优于基线。

Conclusion: 熵率调度可作为高维桥和流采样器在低预算下的实用分配信号。

Abstract: For a fixed flow-based generative model under a small inference budget, sample quality can depend strongly on where the sampler spends its few function evaluations. Flow matching and Schrödinger bridges define probability paths, yet their inference grids are usually heuristic or inherited from one-endpoint diffusion. We derive a conditional-marginal entropy-rate objective for bridge-aware discretization, separating endpoint-conditioned bridge geometry from marginal flow evolution, and use it to build a training-free entropic inference-time scheduler from first principles. For Gaussian Brownian bridges this rate is closed-form and U-shaped, motivating boundary-heavy nonuniform grids. On trained two-dimensional bridge/flow models, the estimated profile recovers the predicted shape and improves 10-step ODE-Heun MMD over linear by 18.1%, with a paired 22.7% SDE-Heun improvement in the same low-NFE sweep. On EDM/CIFAR-10, the entropic time-discretization gives the best tested five-step FID (186.3 \pm 4.0 versus 200.5 \pm 2.9 for linear and 238.0 \pm 5.3 for cosine). On AlphaFlow protein generation, entropic conditional-marginal (cond-marg) scheduling shows advantage in low-NFE regimes on both CAMEO22 and ATLAS benchmarks. These results support entropy-rate scheduling as a practical low-budget allocation signal for high-dimensional bridge and flow samplers.

</details>


### [139] [Navigating Potholes with Geometry-Aware Sharpness Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16134)
*Simon Dufort-Labbé,Mehrab Hamidi,Razvan Pascanu,Ioannis Mitliagkas,Damien Scieur,Aristide Baratin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLQR+SAM通过结合SAM的锐度感知最小化与LLQR的逐层线性二次型调节器预条件器，利用双时间尺度结构（慢速学习几何先验+快速锐度校正），在平坦盆地稳定性的同时增强对局部尖锐区域的逃逸信号，在视觉和序列建模任务上优于单独使用SAM或LLQR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAM对所有参数方向均匀扰动，忽略了损失曲面的几何结构，导致对局部几何信息的利用不足。

Method: 将LLQR框架中的预条件器作为慢速指数移动平均稀疏更新，捕捉平滑的低分辨率损失几何；在此几何基础上进行SAM快速扰动探测曲率，形成双时间尺度优化结构。

Result: 在标准视觉和序列建模基准上，LLQR+SAM一致优于单独使用SAM或LLQR。

Conclusion: 慢速学习的损失几何信息与快速锐度校正确实具有互补性，预条件器能在平坦盆地保持稳定的同时放大对局部尖锐区域（potholes）的逃逸信号。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) encourages flat minima by perturbing parameters along directions of high loss curvature, but treats all parameter directions uniformly, ignoring the underlying loss geometry. We introduce LLQR+SAM, which combines SAM with a learned preconditioner obtained from the recently proposed LLQR framework, a second-order method that recasts steepest descent as a layerwise linear-quadratic regulator problem. The preconditioner is updated sparsely and maintained as a slow exponential moving average, so it captures a smoothed, low-resolution picture of the loss landscape geometry. The SAM perturbation then operates on top of this learned geometry, probing curvature at a faster timescale. We show that this two-timescale structure is not merely a computational convenience: theoretically, the preconditioner amplifies the SAM escape signal in directions that are flat under the average geometry but locally sharp (potholes). Wide, flat basins, by contrast, remain stable. Empirically, LLQR+SAM gives consistent gains over both SAM and LLQR alone across standard vision and sequence modeling benchmarks, supporting the view that slow learned geometry and fast sharpness correction are genuinely complementary.

</details>


### [140] [Entropic Auto-Encoding via Implicit Free-Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16164)
*Hazhir Aliahmadi,Irina Babayan,Greg van Anders*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出熵自编码器（EAE），通过最小化自由能的编码器集成隐式生成先验，仅使用重构损失作为显式目标，有效缓解了变分自编码器（VAE）中的后验坍塌问题，学习到非高斯、多模态的隐变量分布，并在反应扩散过程、MNIST和CelebA数据集上验证了其生成多样性和结构保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分自编码器（VAE）普遍存在后验坍塌问题，即隐变量被有效忽略，原因是显式先验的施加将优化推向对应无信息隐表示的损失景观区域。

Method: 提出熵自编码器（EAE），重构损失是唯一显式目标，通过自由能最小化的编码器集成隐式生成隐变量的先验。该集成将学习偏向近最优解的高容量区域，而解码器更新将搜索轨迹引向信息性隐表示。

Result: EAE缓解了后验坍塌，学习到非高斯、多模态的隐变量分布，生成多样且数据一致的样本，保持数据中不同形式的底层结构。在反应扩散过程中捕获了已知低维动力学的叠加，在MNIST中识别出隐式类别区分，在CelebA上展示了从"全人类"面部到个体依赖特征的分层理解。

Conclusion: EAE作为一种无需显式先验的框架，通过隐式先验机制有效克服了VAE的后验坍塌问题，能够在多个数据集上学习到信息丰富且具有结构感知能力的隐表示。

Abstract: Despite their ubiquity, variational autoencoders (VAEs) inherently suffer from posterior collapse, a failure mode in which latent variables are effectively ignored. This failure arises because explicit prior imposition drives optimization toward loss landscape regions corresponding to uninformative latent representations. Here, we introduce Entropic Autoencoders (EAEs), a framework in which reconstruction loss is the only explicit objective, and entropy generates the latent variables' prior implicitly through a free energy-minimizing ensemble of encoders. This ensemble biases learning toward high-volume regions of near-optimal solutions, while decoder updates direct the search trajectories toward informative latent representations. We demonstrate that EAEs mitigate posterior collapse by learning non-Gaussian, multimodal latent distributions that yield diverse, data-consistent generations and preserve different forms of underlying structure in the data. As a proof-of-concept, we show that an EAE captures a superposition of the known low-dimensional dynamics of a reaction-diffusion process. Then, we show that an EAE identifies implicit categorical distinctions in MNIST latent representations, and displays a hierarchical understanding of facial structure on the CelebA dataset, from an "all-human" face to individual-dependent features.

</details>


### [141] [BAPR: Bayesian amnesic piecewise-robust reinforcement learning for non-stationary continuous control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16170)
*Yifan Zhang,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BAPR算法，将贝叶斯在线变化检测(BOCD)与鲁棒集成强化学习结合，解决分段稳定环境下的策略保守性与自适应性的权衡问题。论文通过Lean 4形式化验证了算子的收缩性质，并推导了切换后恢复的形式化误差边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际控制系统常处于分段稳定状态，标准鲁棒RL方法面临困境：全局保守策略浪费稳定期性能，局部自适应策略在未检测到的机制切换时可能造成灾难性失败。

Method: 1) BAPR算子：模式条件Bellman算子的凸组合，由冻结的信念分布加权，被证明是γ-压缩算子；2) 基于BOCD的自适应保守机制：检测到变化点后策略最大保守，随置信度增长平滑放松，检测延迟O(log(1/δ))；3) 通过Lean 4形式化验证算子的收缩性质及边界条件（γ+λΔ≥1时收缩失败）；4) 上下文条件模块通过RMDM损失训练，训练时使用模拟器提供的模式ID，部署时无需模式标签。

Result: 推导了切换后恢复的组件式形式化误差边界；所有结果在Lean 4中形式化验证（1,145行代码，3个文件，22个机器验证定理），无任何sorry。

Conclusion: BAPR统一了在线变化检测与鲁棒集成RL，通过冻结信念设计实现了可验证的收缩保证和误差边界，在分段稳定环境中兼顾保守性与自适应性。

Abstract: Real-world control systems frequently operate under \emph{piecewise stationary} conditions, where dynamics remain stable for extended periods before undergoing abrupt regime changes. Standard robust RL methods face a fundamental dilemma: a globally conservative policy wastes performance during stable periods, while a locally adaptive policy risks catastrophic failure when the regime changes undetected.
  We propose \textbf{BAPR} (Bayesian Amnesic Piecewise-Robust SAC), which unifies Bayesian Online Change Detection (BOCD) with robust ensemble RL. The BAPR operator -- a convex combination of mode-conditional Bellman operators weighted by a frozen belief distribution -- is a $γ$-contraction. A complementary counterexample, machine-verified in Lean~4, establishes a \emph{sharp boundary}: when beliefs depend on the Q-function, the contraction factor becomes $γ+ λΔ$ (where $Δ$ is the mode reward gap), and contraction fails exactly when $γ+ λΔ\geq 1$. We derive a \emph{component-wise} formal error budget for the abstract operator -- every component machine-verified -- bounding post-switch recovery; the budget applies to the abstract mode-mixture operator and inherits to the implemented shared-critic algorithm only through the frozen-parameter design intuition. All results are formally verified with no \texttt{sorry} (1,145 lines across 3 Lean~4 files, 22 machine-verified theorems). BOCD drives an adaptive conservatism mechanism: the policy becomes maximally conservative after detected change-points and smoothly relaxes as confidence grows, with detection delay $O(\log(1/δ))$. A context-conditioning module trained via RMDM loss provides mode-aware representations from simulator-provided mode IDs at training time and requires no mode labels at deployment.

</details>


### [142] [Imitation learning for clinical decision support in pediatric ECMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16175)
*Fateme Golivand,Michael Skinner,Saurabh Mathur,Ameet Soni,Phillip Reeder,Kristian Kersting,Lakshmi Raman,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用TabPFN（基于Transformer的表格数据方法）在儿科ECMO中学习未直接观测到的临床行动模型，通过模仿学习解决高复杂性和数据稀缺问题，实验表明TabPFN优于XGBoost和MLP等传统基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿科重症监护（尤其是ECMO）具有高复杂性和数据稀缺性，且临床决策中的行动无法直接观测，现有方法难以有效建模，亟需一种能从未观测行动轨迹中学习的决策支持方法。

Method: 将临床决策建模为从轨迹中学习行动的模仿学习问题，采用TabPFN（基于Transformer的表格数据方法）学习行动模型，并与XGBoost、MLP等传统基线在真实儿科ECMO数据上进行比较。

Result: TabPFN在真实儿科ECMO数据上 consistently outperforms XGBoost和MLP等传统基线方法。

Conclusion: TabPFN可作为儿科ECMO决策支持中强有力的临床行为基线模型。

Abstract: Pediatric critical care is a dynamic, high-stakes process involving constant monitoring and adjustments in life-saving treatments. Modeling these interventions is crucial for effective decision support. To address the challenges of high complexity and data scarcity in pediatric Extracorporeal Membrane Oxygenation (ECMO), we frame clinical decision-making as learning to act from trajectories, i.e., imitation learning that learns action models from observational data, with a key feature that actions are not directly observed. We consider TabPFN, a recent transformer-based approach for tabular data, and traditional baselines including XGBoost and Multi-Layer Perceptrons(MLPs) on real-world pediatric ECMO data to learn the action models. We find that the TabPFN-based approach consistently outperforms these classical baselines, supporting its use as a strong clinician-behavior baseline for pediatric ECMO decision support.

</details>


### [143] [Hypothesis-driven construction of mesoscopic dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16211)
*Zhuoyuan Li,Aiqing Zhu,Qianxiao Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于广义昂萨格原理的数学约束假设类框架，通过学习介观动力学替代传统科学建模，该框架在训练前即可为所有时空演化方程提供全局适定性、渐近稳定性、唯一分解可识别性和离散能量耗散等理论保证，并通过实例数据识别假设类成员，从而获得准确、鲁棒且可解释的动力学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学建模从固定的、实例层面的有效方程出发，然后进行特定方程的分析和计算，这一过程在复杂应用（如多尺度系统）中变得异常困难。因此需要一种替代范式来克服这些挑战。

Method: 基于广义昂萨格原理，构建包含耗散和保守介观动力学的统一数学约束假设类框架；在训练前建立全局适定性、渐近稳定性、唯一分解可识别性和离散能量耗散等先验理论保证；通过实例数据指导假设类成员识别。

Result: 在连续PDE模型数据和微观链模型数据（其精确介观尺度模型未知）上均进行了实证验证，该方法不仅能有效学习动力学，还能提供可解释的底层物理诊断信息。

Conclusion: 该框架是一种有效的动力学学习方法，同时能提供对底层物理的可解释诊断，为复杂多尺度系统的建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Traditional scientific modeling typically begins with fixed, instance-wise effective equations and then carries out equation-specific analysis and computation, a procedure that becomes exceptionally challenging in complex applications such as multiscale systems. We propose an alternative paradigm by learning mesoscopic dynamics within a mathematically constrained hypothesis class. Building upon a generalized Onsager principle, we introduce a unified framework encompassing both dissipative and conservative mesoscopic dynamics. We establish uniform and a priori theoretical guarantees, including global well-posedness, asymptotic stability, unique factorization identifiability, and discrete energy dissipation, applicable to all spatio-temporal evolution equations within this hypothesis class prior to all learning stages. Data from each problem instance is then used to guide the identification of members within our hypothesis class, giving rise to accurate, robust and interpretable dynamical models. We empirically validate this framework on both data from continuum PDE models as a check, and on data arising from microscopic chain models for which exact meso-scale models are unknown. The proposed approach not only acts as an effective dynamics learner, but also offers vital interpretable diagnostics of the underlying physics.

</details>


### [144] [The Privacy Price of Tail-Risk Learning: Effective Tail Sample Size in Differentially Private CVaR Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16219)
*El Mustapha Mansouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了差分隐私下条件风险价值（CVaR）学习问题的样本复杂度，揭示了隐私机制下有效样本量由$n\tau$决定（而非$n$），并给出了标量估计、有限类以及凸Lipschitz学习场景下的完全收敛速率，指出私有CVaR学习的核心难子问题等价于在$\Theta(n\tau)$个尾部记录上进行普通私有学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在差分隐私约束下学习CVaR（尾部风险度量）时，传统样本复杂度分析不再适用，需要厘清隐私预算、尾部质量与样本量之间的交互关系，并建立完整的上下界理论。

Method: 通过理论推导建立CVaR学习的精细误差分解（普通尾部风险统计误差 + 隐私代价），分别对标量估计、有限假设类（大小$M$）和凸Lipschitz学习三类场景进行上下界分析，并利用纯DP与近似DP（小$\delta$）下的归约论证得到完整收敛速率。

Result: 标量估计的速率为$\Theta(B\min\{1,(n\tau)^{-1/2}+(\varepsilon n\tau)^{-1}\})$；有限类（大小$M$）的速率为$\Theta(B\min\{1,\sqrt{\log(2M)/(n\tau)}+\log(2M)/(\varepsilon n\tau)\})$；凸Lipschitz学习中隐私项必然以$1/(\varepsilon n\tau)$尺度增长，维度依赖性与私有随机凸优化一致。

Conclusion: 私有CVaR学习的核心难子问题等价于在$\Theta(n\tau)$个信息性尾部记录上进行普通私有学习；隐私相关的有效样本量由尾部质量$\tau$缩放决定。

Abstract: Differential privacy changes the effective sample size governing CVaR learning. For tail mass $τ$, the privacy-relevant sample size is not $n$, but $nτ$; equivalently, the effective private tail sample size is $εnτ$. Private CVaR excess risk decomposes into ordinary tail-risk statistical error and a privacy price. This decomposition is complete for scalar estimation and finite classes: scalar estimation has rate $Θ(B \min\{1,(nτ)^{-1/2}+(εnτ)^{-1}\})$, and finite classes of size $M$ have rate $Θ(B \min\{1,\sqrt{\log(2M)/(nτ)}+\log(2M)/(εnτ)\})$. These complete rates hold under pure DP, and their lower bounds extend to approximate DP in the stated small-$δ$ regimes. For convex Lipschitz learning, modular upper and lower reductions show that the CVaR-specific privacy term necessarily scales as $1/(εnτ)$, with dimension dependence inherited from private stochastic convex optimization. Together, these results identify ordinary private learning on $Θ(nτ)$ informative tail records as the canonical hard subproblem inside private CVaR learning.

</details>


### [145] [Layer Equivalence Is Not a Property of Layers Alone: How You Test Redundancy Changes What You Find](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16234)
*Gabriel Garcia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了Transformer层间两种等价性测试（替换测试与交换测试）在压缩评估中存在系统性差异，该差异随训练进程增大，且在不同架构下对剪枝安全性的影响不同，建议在剪枝前同时评估两种指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
