Motivation: 现有研究在判断Transformer层是否"等价"时，混淆了替换（Replacement）和交换（Interchange）两种不同的测试协议，导致层压缩的安全性评估不一致，需要系统量化这种协议差异。

Method: 在Pythia（410M/1.4B）、Qwen3-8B、Llama-3.1-8B等模型上，测量替换KL散度与交换KL散度，比较两种协议下的层间距离及其对剪枝结果的影响。

Result: 1) 替换-交换差距从初始化到收敛逐渐扩大；2) Qwen3-8B中交换引导剪枝远优于替换引导；3) Llama-3.1-8B中两种协议剪枝代价相同，但交换KL更低，说明指标差距不一定映射到移除效果。

Conclusion: 两种swap-KL协议在层压缩中给出不同信号，建议在层移除或合并前同时评估两者，该诊断仅需无标签前向传播。

Abstract: When researchers ask whether two transformer layers are "equivalent" for compression, they often conflate distinct tests. Replacement asks whether one layer's map can substitute for another's in place; interchange asks whether two layers approximately commute when their positions are swapped. Both are output-grounded swap-KL probes, but they need not agree: on pretrained transformers the protocol gap can change which layers look safe to prune by several-fold under the same evaluator, especially when replacement distances are high.
  We measure both protocols across checkpoints and architectures. On a Pythia training trajectory (410M and 1.4B), the replacement-interchange gap grows from initialization to convergence. Under one matched WikiText-2 contract at 8B scale, Qwen3-8B enters a divergent regime: interchange-guided removal is several-fold safer than replacement-guided at the same layer budgets, while Llama-3.1-8B ties the two protocols for pruning cost even though interchange KL is lower, showing metric gaps need not map one-to-one to removal. Before layer removal or merging, score both swap-KLs on the target checkpoint; the diagnostic requires only unlabeled forward passes.

</details>


### [146] [Dynamics-Level Watermarking of Flow Matching Models with Random Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16239)
*Shuchan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于动力学层面的生成模型水印方法，将水印直接嵌入到流匹配模型的连续动力学（速度场）中，通过密钥相关的扰动实现消息编码，在不改变生成分布的前提下实现可靠的消息恢复和黑盒检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统水印方法将信号嵌入模型权重或输出中，本文探索一种新的动力学层面的水印嵌入方式，旨在实现更隐蔽、更鲁棒的水印机制。

Method: 将水印问题建模为连续信道上的随机编码：在训练过程中添加密钥相关的扰动到速度场中，检测时通过黑盒查询恢复消息。扰动设计确保不影响生成的分布。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上，不同架构下均验证了可靠的消息恢复能力，生成质量未受损，且没有密钥时解码准确率仅为随机水平。

Conclusion: 动力学层面的水印嵌入是可行的，能够在不影响生成质量的前提下实现可靠的消息恢复，并具备密钥依赖性（安全性）。

Abstract: We introduce a dynamics-level approach to watermarking generative models. Rather than embedding signals into model weights or outputs, we embed the watermark directly into the learned continuous dynamics -- the velocity field of a flow matching model. We formulate this as random coding over a continuous channel: a key-dependent perturbation is added during training, and the message is recovered at detection time from black-box queries. The perturbation is designed to leave the generated distribution unchanged. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 across different architectures confirm reliable message recovery, preserved generation quality, and chance-level decoding accuracy without the secret key.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [147] [Multi-Agent Cooperative Transportation: Optimal and Efficient Task Allocation and Path Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16097)
*Ning Zhou,Nikolai W. F. Bode,Edmund R. Hunt*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文正式定义了协同运输任务分配与路径规划（CT-TAPF）问题，提出了最优求解器CT-TCBS（包含增量扩展策略）以及一系列次优求解器，实验表明增量扩展策略有效剪枝搜索空间，并识别了任务-冲突扩展困境，次优求解器在解质量-运行时间谱系上建立了更高效的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架如MAPF和TAPF缺乏真正的多智能体协同能力，无法处理需要多个智能体共同运输大件物品的任务，因此需要一种集成团队组建、任务分配和无碰撞路径规划的新问题形式化与求解方法。

Method: 提出CT-TAPF问题形式化；设计最优求解器CT-TCBS，采用增量扩展策略应对团队组建的组合爆炸；开发基于全局任务视角的次优求解器，按全局难度指标（最优任务/最差任务）选择下一步任务。

Result: （1）增量扩展策略显著优于朴素组合方法，成功剪枝了主导的任务分配搜索空间；（2）识别了任务-冲突扩展困境：在大智能体路径规划子问题中有效的复杂冲突解决器在CT-TAPF集成设定中可能有害；（3）次优求解器在解质量-运行时间谱系上建立了优于基于智能体基线的更高效边界。

Conclusion: 本文为新型实用的协同多智能体问题提供了基础框架和一系列有效算法。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems are integral to modern logistics, but their capabilities are often limited to tasks executable by individual agents. This paper addresses a critical gap in existing frameworks like Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) and Task Allocation and Path Finding (TAPF), which lack true cooperation for transporting large items that require multiple agents. To this end, we formalise the Cooperative Transportation Task Allocation and Path Finding (CT-TAPF) problem, which integrates team formation, task assignment, and collision-free pathfinding. We present an optimal solver, Cooperative Transportation Task Conflict-Based Search (CT-TCBS), which features a novel Incremental Expansion strategy to tackle the combinatorial explosion inherent in team formation. Recognising the computational cost of optimality, we also develop a family of sub-optimal solvers that employ a global, task-centric perspective, selecting the next task to assign based on a global difficulty metric (Best Task or Worst Task). Our comprehensive empirical evaluation demonstrates three key findings: (1) the incremental expansion strategy significantly outperforms the naive combinatorial approach by successfully pruning the dominant task-allocation search space; (2) we identify a task-conflict expansion dilemma, where sophisticated conflict resolvers effective for large-agent pathfinding subproblems can be detrimental in the integrated CT-TAPF setting; and (3) our proposed sub-optimal solvers establish a new, more efficient frontier on the solution quality-runtime spectrum compared to "nn-" agent-centric baselines. This work provides a foundational framework and a set of effective algorithms for a new, practical class of cooperative multi-agent problems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Autonomous Intelligent Agents for Natural-Language-Driven Web Execution with Integrated Security Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15281)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种AI驱动的自主测试框架，通过五种集成策略和容器化工作架构，将脚本生成成功率从55%提升至93%，导航故障降低8倍，时序竞态条件消除80%，测试创建时间减少75%，并能自然语言驱动安全测试，检测85%的认证绕过漏洞和95%的输入验证缺陷，假阳性率低于12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Web测试套件容易因UI重构、时序变化等因素快速腐烂，导致开发者放弃维护，亟需一种能自动适应变化、减少人工干预的测试框架。

Method: 提出AI驱动的自主测试框架，包含五种集成策略：导航可靠性、上下文感知的选择器生成、生成后验证、智能等待注入和失败学习；采用容器化工作架构，将编排与长时间运行的浏览器执行解耦。

Result: 在4个生产应用和176个场景中评估：脚本生成成功率从55%提升至93%，导航故障降低8倍，时序竞态条件消除80%，测试创建时间比手动Selenium减少75%；安全检测方面，认证绕过漏洞检测率85%，输入验证缺陷检测率95%，假阳性率低于12%。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了Web测试套件腐烂问题，显著提升了测试生成成功率和可靠性，并首次将自然语言驱动的安全测试引入该领域，实现了从攻击描述到OWASP Top 10对齐的浏览器探针的自动转换。

Abstract: Modern web test suites rot. A UI refactor breaks locators, a timing change causes race conditions, and within weeks developers abandon the suite entirely. This paper presents an AI-driven autonomous testing framework that addresses these failure modes through five integrated strategies - navigation reliability, context-aware selector generation, post-generation validation, smart wait injection, and failure learning - implemented over a containerised worker architecture that decouples orchestration from long-running browser execution. Evaluated across four production applications and 176 scenarios, the framework improves script generation success from 55% to 93%, achieves an 8x reduction in navigation failures, eliminates 80% of timing-related race conditions, and reduces test creation time by 75% compared to manual Selenium authoring. The framework extends naturally to security validation: testers describe attack scenarios in plain English - "try accessing another user's invoice" - which the agent converts to OWASP Top 10-aligned browser probes, detecting 85% of authentication bypass vulnerabilities and 95% of input validation flaws with false positive rates below 12%. Natural-language-driven security testing of this kind represents, to our knowledge, a novel contribution to the field.

</details>


### [149] [Quantum Futures Interactive: A Live Demonstration of Post-Quantum Blockchain Security, Infrastructure Tradeoffs, and Sustainable Distributed Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15991)
*Dongping Liu,Aoyu Zhang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为"Quantum Futures Interactive"的跨学科演示平台，该平台通过教育可视化、参与式互动和密码学工件生成，帮助研究、工程、治理和投资等多个社区理解从经典密码学向量子安全区块链系统的过渡过程，并与联合国可持续发展目标保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的进展对区块链平台和去中心化应用中广泛部署的公钥密码系统构成长期安全威胁，而目前各社区（研究、工程、治理、投资）对量子风险的认知碎片化、不统一，需要一种跨学科的交互式平台来弥合认知鸿沟。

Method: 设计并实现了一个包含量子威胁教育、情感捕获、技术优先级排序、基础设施权衡探索以及后量子密码输出生成的结构化交互流程平台，整合了分布式信任、可持续性基础设施和负责任创新等概念。

Result: 该演示平台成功实现了跨学科对话，参与者能够通过互动流程理解量子风险、表达观点、探索技术权衡，并生成后量子密码学工件，同时与联合国可持续发展目标相对接。

Conclusion: 该平台有效促进了区块链韧性方面的跨学科对话，为从经典密码学向量子安全区块链系统的过渡提供了交互式决策框架，并强调了可持续性和负责任创新的重要性。

Abstract: Advances in quantum computing introduce long-term security challenges for widely deployed public-key cryptographic systems used across blockchain platforms and decentralized applications. Although post-quantum cryptography (PQC) standards are emerging, understanding quantum risk remains fragmented across research, engineering, governance, and investment communities. This demo presents Quantum Futures Interactive, a live interdisciplinary demonstration platform combining educational visualization, participatory interaction, and cryptographic artifact generation to illustrate the transition from classical to quantum-resilient blockchain systems. Participants engage in a structured interaction flow including quantum threat education, sentiment capture, technology prioritization, infrastructure tradeoff exploration, and generation of post-quantum cryptographic outputs. The system integrates distributed trust concepts, sustainability-aware infrastructure considerations, and responsible innovation within an interactive decision framework. The demonstration supports interdisciplinary dialogue on blockchain resilience while aligning with United Nations Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs).

</details>


### [150] [Distance-Preserving Digests: A Primitive for BFT Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15329)
*Ryan Patrick Mercier*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种距离保持的事务摘要（distance-preserving transaction digests）原语，用8维空间中的交换向量和替代碰撞抵抗哈希，从而消除BFT共识中因哈希碰撞抵抗导致的距离信息丢失。基于该原语设计了三个应用：Proxima（两阶段BFT协议，一致时单轮终结）、树结构共识（每组仅10个验证者）和跨分片一致性验证（每对分片128字节）。安全性证明显示少于N/3拜占庭验证者无法导致冲突最终确认。在N=100,000时，Proxima Tree消息量仅为HotStuff的2.2倍，单核终结时间0.9秒对比18秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BFT共识协议依赖碰撞抵抗哈希来比较验证者状态，但碰撞抵抗特性破坏了距离信息：两个在19/20交易上一致的验证者会产生不相关的哈希，与完全不一致的验证者无法区分。这迫使BFT文献中出现三个设计约束：验证者必须在投票前同步状态、协议执行完成前无法衡量一致性质量、层级委员会必须足够大以支持独立BFT从而限制树深度。本文旨在通过设计一种保持距离的交易摘要原语来打破这些约束。

Method: 提出距离保持的事务摘要原语，用8维空间中的交换向量和替代碰撞抵抗哈希。该原语具备哈希所没有的三个属性：距离与不一致程度成正比、加权均值精确、可通过布隆过滤器差异识别集合差异。基于此设计了三个应用：(1) Proxima——两阶段BFT协议，一致时单轮终结；(2) 树结构共识——每组仅10个验证者（对比以太坊的128个），通过距离过滤替代每组BFT；(3) 跨分片一致性验证——每对分片128字节，替代两阶段提交的每交易协调。安全性证明：少于N/3拜占庭验证者无法导致冲突最终确认，与阶段1聚类或树拓扑无关。

Result: 在N=100,000规模下，Proxima Tree相较于HotStuff消息量减少2.2倍（该结构特性不受并行化影响）。单核实现中终结时间0.9秒对比HotStuff的18秒；多核BLS方案可缩小但无法消除该差距。

Conclusion: 距离保持摘要原语有效解决了碰撞抵抗哈希导致的距离信息丢失问题，显著降低了BFT共识的通信开销和延迟，同时保持了安全性。该工作为BFT协议设计提供了新的基础原语和架构思路。

Abstract: Every BFT consensus protocol uses collision-resistant hashes to compare validator state. Collision resistance destroys distance: two validators agreeing on 19 of 20 transactions produce unrelated hashes, indistinguishable from validators sharing nothing. This forces three design constraints across the BFT literature: validators must synchronize state before voting, agreement quality cannot be measured until votes are counted, and hierarchical committees must be large enough for independent BFT, limiting tree depth. This paper introduces distance-preserving transaction digests, a primitive that replaces collision-resistant hashes with commutative vector sums in 8-dimensional space. The primitive has three properties hashes lack: distance is proportional to disagreement, weighted means are exact, and set differences are identifiable via bloom filter diff. We demonstrate three applications: a two-phase BFT protocol (Proxima) that achieves single-round finality when validators agree; tree-structured consensus with groups of 10 validators (vs 128 in Ethereum), enabled because distance filtering replaces per-group BFT; and cross-shard consistency verification at 128 bytes per shard pair, replacing the per-transaction coordination of two-phase commit. Safety is proved: fewer than N/3 Byzantine validators cannot cause conflicting finalization, independent of Phase 1 clustering or tree topology. At N =100,000, Proxima Tree uses 2.2x fewer messages than HotStuff (a structural property unaffected by parallelism). Single-core finality is 0.9s vs 18s for HotStuff; multi-core BLS narrows but does not eliminate this gap.

</details>


### [151] [Hidden in Memory: Sleeper Memory Poisoning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15338)
*Sidharth Pulipaka,Stanislau Hlebik,Leonidas Raghav,Sahar Abdelnabi,Vyas Raina,Ivaxi Sheth,Mario Fritz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了“休眠记忆中毒”攻击：通过向外部上下文中注入对抗性内容，使大语言模型助手的持久化记忆被篡改。该攻击可延迟触发并在后续多轮对话中持续影响模型行为。实验表明，GPT-5.5和Kimi-K2.6上被投毒记忆的写入率分别高达99.8%和95%，在成功检索的情况下，60-89%的评估中模型执行了攻击者意图的动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM助手引入持久化记忆机制以支持个性化与跨会话连续性，新的安全风险随之出现——对抗性内容可污染助手的记忆，从而影响未来交互。现有研究主要关注即时提示注入，缺乏对延迟触发、跨会话持久影响的攻击形式的系统研究。

Method: 提出了“休眠记忆中毒”攻击范式，攻击者通过操纵外部上下文（如文档、网页或仓库）使助手存储关于用户的伪造记忆。攻击流程分为三步：毒化记忆写入、后续检索、以及最终利用记忆引导对话。在GPT-5.5和Kimi-K2.6等多个有状态LLM助手系统上进行了全流程评估。

Result: GPT-5.5上毒化记忆写入成功率达99.8%，Kimi-K2.6上为95%；在成功检索到毒化记忆的场景中，攻击者意图的智能体动作在60-89%的评估中被模型执行。

Conclusion: 持久化记忆机制构成了一个长期攻击面，可在多轮未来对话中持续被利用。现有安全防护不足以防御此类延迟触发的记忆中毒攻击。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly augmented with persistent memory, allowing assistants to store user-specific information across sessions for personalization and continuity. This statefulness introduces a new security risk: adversarial content can corrupt what an assistant remembers and thereby influence future interactions. We propose and study sleeper memory poisoning, a delayed attack in which an adversary manipulates external context, such as a document, webpage, or repository, to cause the assistant to store a fabricated memory about the user. Unlike conventional prompt injection, the attack can remain dormant and re-emerge across multiple later conversations. We evaluate the full attack pipeline: whether poisoned memories are written, later retrieved, and ultimately used to steer the following conversations. Across stateful LLM assistants, poisoned memories were added up to 99.8% on GPT-5.5 and 95% on Kimi-K2.6. Crucially, among successful retrievals, poisoned memories cause attacker-intended agentic actions in 60-89% of evaluations across models. These results show that persistent memory can act as a long-term attack surface across multiple future conversations.

</details>


### [152] [uGen: An Agentic Framework for Generating Microarchitectural Attack PoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15503)
*Debopriya Roy Dipta,Thore Tiemann,Eduard Marin,Thomas Eisenbarth,Berk Gulmezoglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 uGen，首个基于大语言模型（LLM）驱动的微架构攻击代码自动生成框架。通过检索增强的多智能体设计，弥补 LLM 在攻击原语方面的知识缺失，能够根据防御者需求自动生成功能正确的微架构攻击概念验证代码（PoC）。在多种微架构和攻击类型上，uGen 实现了最高 100%（Spectre-v1）和 80%（Prime+Probe）的成功率，生成成本仅需 1.25 美元，耗时不到 4 分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有微架构攻击评估面临攻击代码开发劳动密集、需深厚微架构专业知识、对环境高度敏感且缺乏可移植性的问题。尽管 LLM 有降低这些门槛的潜力，但其能否可靠生成功能正确的微架构攻击代码尚不明确。本文旨在系统性地解决这一挑战。

Method: 首先系统研究 GPT、Claude、Qwen3 等前沿模型，发现 LLM 频繁错误生成或错放关键攻击原语。基于此分析，uGen 采用检索增强的多智能体架构，注入缺失的领域知识，合成符合防御者需求的微架构攻击 PoC 代码。

Result: 在多种微架构、漏洞函数和 LLM 平台上的评估显示：在部署阶段，uGen 对 Spectre-v1（Claude Sonnet-4）的成功率达 100%，Prime+Probe（Qwen3-Coder）成功率为 80%，生成一个成功 PoC 代码的成本为 1.25 美元，耗时不到 4 分钟。

Conclusion: uGen 证明了 LLM 驱动的自动化微架构攻击代码生成的可行性和有效性，为系统化、可扩展的微架构漏洞评估提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Microarchitectural attacks continue to evolve, uncovering new exploitation vectors in modern processors. From a defensive perspective, assessing a system's susceptibility to such attacks remains challenging. Developing functional attack implementations is labor-intensive, requires deep microarchitectural expertise, and is highly sensitive to execution environments. Consequently, existing attacks often lack portability, limiting systematic and scalable vulnerability assessment. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) suggest a potential avenue for lowering these barriers. However, it remains unclear whether LLMs can reliably generate functionally correct microarchitectural attack code suitable for rigorous vulnerability testing.
  In this work, we present uGen, the first LLM-driven framework for automated microarchitectural attack code generation. A key challenge we address is identifying attack-specific knowledge gaps in LLMs. Through a systematic study of state-of-the-art models (GPT, Claude, and Qwen3), we find that LLMs frequently misgenerate or misplace critical attack primitives. Guided by this analysis, uGen employs a retrieval-augmented, multi-agent design that injects missing domain knowledge to synthesize functionally correct microarchitectural attack PoCs tailored to defender requirements. We evaluate uGen on cache-based and speculative-execution attacks across diverse set of microarchitectures, vulnerable functions, and LLM platforms. In the deployment stage, uGen achieves up to 100% success rate for Spectre-v1 (Claude Sonnet-4) and 80% for Prime+Probe (Qwen3-Coder). Finally, we demonstrate that uGen can generate a successful PoC code with a cost of $1.25 in under four minutes.

</details>


### [153] [Detecting Privilege Escalation in Polyglot Microservices via Agentic Program Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15569)
*Penghui Li,Hong Yau Chong,Yinzhi Cao,Junfeng Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Neo是一个将大语言模型与经典程序分析相结合的智能体框架，用于检测微服务架构中的权限提升漏洞。在25个开源微服务应用（620万行代码，7种编程语言）上，Neo发现了24个零日权限提升漏洞，精度81.0%，召回率85.0%，并扩展到其他领域发现了18个额外零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构中复杂的跨服务交互、多语言代码库以及多样化的特权操作与权限检查，使得检测权限提升漏洞极具挑战性，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: Neo提出基于LLM的智能体框架，动态生成分析计划、自适应调整代码搜索策略并验证语义；同时开发了代码搜索原语，实现跨服务、跨语言的可扩展且灵活的代码探索。

Result: 在25个开源微服务应用（6.2M代码行，7种编程语言）上发现24个零日权限提升漏洞，精度81.0%，召回率85.0%；扩展到其他领域发现18个额外零日漏洞。

Conclusion: Neo在检测精度和可扩展性上显著优于现有程序分析和智能体方案，并展示了良好的可扩展性，可应用于其他应用领域和漏洞类型。

Abstract: Microservices are widely adopted in modern cloud systems due to their scalability and fault tolerance. However, microservice architectures introduce significant complexity in privilege and permission control, creating risks of privilege escalation where attackers can gain unauthorized access to resources or operations. Detecting such vulnerabilities is challenging due to complex cross-service interactions, polyglot codebases, and diverse privileged operations and permission checks. We present Neo, an agentic program analysis framework that combines large language models (LLMs) with classic program analysis to address these challenges. Neo leverages an LLM-based agent that dynamically generates analysis plans, adapts code search strategies, and validates semantics. We develop code search primitives that enable Neo to perform scalable and flexible code exploration across services and languages. We evaluated Neo on 25 open-source microservice applications spanning 7 programming languages and 6.2 million lines of code. Neo uncovered 24 zero-day privilege escalation vulnerabilities and achieved 81.0% precision and 85.0% recall on a ground-truth dataset. Compared to existing program analysis and agentic solutions, Neo demonstrated significant improvements in both detection accuracy and scalability. We further showcased Neo's extensibility by applying it to other application domains and vulnerability types, uncovering 18 additional zero-day vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [154] [Compositional Jailbreaking: An Empirical Analysis of Mutator Chain Interactions in Aligned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15598)
*Reinelle Jan Bugnot,Soohyeon Choi,Hoon Wei Lim,Yue Duan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了弱越狱攻击的突变器链式组合（mutator chaining）现象，揭示了多数组合存在破坏性干扰或结构不兼容，仅少数组合产生协同增效作用，同时发现了单策略评估无法揭示的安全对齐结构性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有手工和自动越狱攻击研究，但简单攻击之间的组合交互（特别是弱攻击的序贯组合）尚未被充分探索，理解这些交互机制对构建更鲁棒的安全防御至关重要。

Method: 实现12个基线突变器，在三个主流大语言模型上评估所有有序对组合，引入衡量转换持久性和攻击有效性的完整性与有效性指标。

Result: 组合交互呈现高度非均匀性：大多数组合未能超越单个突变器，表现出破坏性干扰或结构不兼容；仅少数组合产生协同效应，提升攻击成功率；同时揭示了安全对齐的结构性属性。

Conclusion: 对抗性提示组合存在细微的动态交互机制，多数组合无效但少数具有增效作用，失败模式可揭示安全对齐的结构性缺陷，为构建更强健的防御提供新见解。

Abstract: Jailbreaking attacks on large language models pose a significant threat to AI safety by enabling the generation of harmful or restricted content. While prior work has explored both handcrafted and automated jailbreak strategies, the potential for compositional interaction between simple attacks remains underexplored. This paper presents a systematic study of mutator chaining, in which weak jailbreak transformations are applied sequentially to characterize how they interact: whether they reinforce one another, interfere destructively, or produce no meaningful change. We implement twelve baseline mutators and evaluate all ordered pairs on a benchmark of harmful prompts against three popular LLM models. Our framework introduces metrics for completeness and validity that capture both transformation persistence and attack effectiveness. Results reveal that the interaction landscape is highly non-uniform, while most combinations fail to outperform individual mutators, exhibiting destructive interference or structural incompatibility, a small fraction produce synergistic effects that improve attack success rates. Equally important, the prevalent failure modes reveal structural properties of safety alignment that are not apparent from single-strategy evaluations. These findings highlight the nuanced dynamics of adversarial prompt composition and offer new insights for building more robust safety defenses.

</details>


### [155] [Rethinking the Security of DP-SGD: A Corrected Analysis of Differentially Private Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15648)
*Wenhao Wang,Shujie Cui,Hui Cui,Xingliang Yuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现DP-SGD的实际实现（如Meta的Opacus）与理论分析之间存在不匹配：标准分析基于子采样高斯机制（SGM），但实际实现中常增加归一化步骤（除以期望批次大小或实际批次大小），导致形式化为期望平均SGM（EASGM）或批次平均SGM（ASGM）。理论证明这些变体的隐私保证弱于标准SGM，并通过对四种SOTA实现（包括Opacus v0.9.0至v1.5.4）的实证审计验证了隐私泄露超出SGM保证，最终为最新实现提供了修正的隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP-SGD的隐私分析通常建模为SGM（加噪到子采样批次梯度之和），但实际工程实现中普遍存在的归一化步骤（除以批次大小）未被理论覆盖，导致正式隐私保证与实际隐私泄露之间可能存在差距。

Method: 重新形式化DP-SGD为EASGM（除以期望批次大小）和ASGM（除以实际批次大小）；理论推导两种变体的隐私曲线；对Meta Opacus等四种实现进行隐私审计。

Result: 理论证明EASGM和ASGM的隐私保证在某些场景下弱于标准SGM；实证审计发现实际隐私泄露超出SGM保证的理论界；对Opacus v0.9.0~v1.5.4的审计揭示了隐私退化趋势。

Conclusion: DP-SGD实际实现的归一化步骤会导致隐私保证被高估，需采用修正后的隐私分析；为最新Opacus版本提供了修正的隐私保证。

Abstract: Differentially Private Stochastic Gradient Descent (DP-SGD) is widely used to protect training data in machine learning. Its privacy guarantee is commonly analyzed through a security game in which an adversary infers whether a target record is included in the training dataset from the mechanism output. The resulting privacy leakage is characterized by a privacy curve, which reports the false negative rate as a function of the false positive rate.
  We identify a mismatch between this formal analysis and common DP-SGD implementations. Existing analyses often model DP-SGD and its variants as the Subsampled Gaussian Mechanism (SGM), where Gaussian noise is added to the sum of clipped gradients computed from a Poisson-sampled batch. In practice, however, many implementations apply an additional normalization step: the noisy gradient sum is divided either by the expected batch size or by the sampled batch size. These mechanisms are therefore better formalized as the Expected-Averaged SGM (EASGM) or the Batch-Averaged SGM (ASGM), respectively.
  We re-analyze the privacy guarantees of DP-SGD under the EASGM and ASGM formulations. Our theoretical results show that these guarantees can be weaker than the standard SGM-based guarantee, implying that the true privacy leakage may exceed the reported guarantee in some regimes. We further audit four state-of-the-art DP-SGD implementations, including Meta's Opacus library, and observe empirical leakage beyond the SGM-based guarantees. Finally, we audit Opacus versions v0.9.0 to v1.5.4 and derive a corrected privacy guarantee for the latest implementation.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Controlled Noise: Achieving Symmetric FHE through Dynamic Position Shifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15774)
*Mostefa Kara*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于明文分片和动态插位机制的对称全同态加密方案，通过双调节器系统（指数调节器和系数调节器）控制乘法过程中的噪声增长，并利用绑定机制保障安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全同态加密方案计算开销巨大且噪声管理复杂，亟需一种更高效、噪声可控的替代方案。

Method: 基于模块化加密基础 c = mk + rp，通过明文分片到不同逻辑位置，引入双调节器系统：指数调节器(t_i)将分片乘积重定向至新目标位置以防止密钥指数累积，系数调节器(d_i)对结果标量进行归一化；通过指数与系数的相互依赖绑定机制抵御代数攻击和替换攻击。

Result: 该方案在保持加法同态性的同时，有效解决了乘法运算中指数级噪声增长的问题。

Conclusion: 提出了一种对称FHE方案，通过分片插位和双调节器机制实现噪声可控的全同态加密，安全性由绑定机制保障。

Abstract: Traditional Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) schemes often suffer from prohibitive computational overhead and complex noise management. In this paper, we propose a novel symmetric FHE through a mechanism of plaintext fragmentation and dynamic interposition. Our approach is built upon a modular encryption foundation, c = mk + rp, which is naturally additive but typically limited by exponential noise growth during multiplication. To resolve this, we introduce an interposition framework where the plaintext is partitioned into multiple fragments across distinct logical positions. We introduce a dual-regulator system to govern the multiplication process; exponent regulators (t_i) redirect the product of fragments to a new target position, preventing the accumulation of secret key exponents, while coefficient regulators (d_i) normalize the resulting scalars. Security is established through a binding mechanism where exponents and coefficients are mutually dependent, shielding the secret key k from algebraic manipulation and substitution attacks.

</details>


### [157] [Security Analysis of a Communication Protocol: MQTT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15804)
*Ricardo Venâncio,Clarisse Sousa,Filipe Duarte,Luís Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了MQTT协议在物联网环境中的安全性，通过理论综述与智能家居仿真实验相结合的方法，执行并分析了窃听、篡改、拒绝服务和暴力破解攻击，揭示了因缺乏强加密和认证导致的严重风险，并提出了相应的缓解策略和最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网中MQTT协议广泛应用，但其安全性存在隐患，论文旨在识别MQTT协议的漏洞并提出安全改进方案。

Method: 采用混合方法论，结合理论综述与智能家居仿真环境中的实验验证，执行了窃听、篡改、DoS和暴力破解四种攻击。

Result: 实验结果表明，由于缺乏强大的加密和认证机制，MQTT协议存在严重安全风险，上述攻击均能成功实施。

Conclusion: 提出了针对性的缓解策略和最佳实践，以加强MQTT协议在实际部署中的安全性。

Abstract: This paper analyzes the security of the Message Queuing Telemetry Transport (MQTT) protocol in the context of the Internet of Things (IoT). The main objective consists of identifying vulnerabilities and proposing security improvements. Adopting a hybrid methodology, a theoretical review was combined with an experimental demonstration in a simulated Smart Home environment. Eavesdropping, Tampering, Denial of Service (DoS), and Brute Force attacks were executed and analyzed. The results evidenced critical risks due to the absence of robust encryption and authentication. Finally, mitigation strategies and best practices are proposed to strengthen MQTT implementations.

</details>


### [158] [FedEDAuth -- Federated Embedding Distribution Authentication for Counterfeit IC Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15885)
*Naseeruddin Lodge,Dhruva Aklekar,Vineet Chadalavada,Nahush Tambe,Sina Gholami,Minhaj Alam,Fareena Saqib*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FedEDAuth，一种基于嵌入分布认证的轻量级客户端验证框架，用于在联邦学习中检测并过滤拜占庭数据投毒攻击者，在50个分布式参与者的实验中实现了100%的恶意客户端检测率和94.17%的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假冒集成电路对电子系统的安全性、可靠性和可信度构成严重威胁。联邦学习虽能为半导体供应链提供隐私保护的协同假冒检测，但其易受拜占庭数据投毒攻击，限制了实际部署。

Method: FedEDAuth利用来自黄金数据集的参考嵌入分布，通过异常值分析、均值偏移测量和微聚类行为评估客户端，无需访问原始数据或梯度，在标准联邦学习流水线中集成。

Result: 在50个分布式参与者、拜占庭数据投毒攻击下，恶意客户端检测率达100%，过滤后的联邦模型假冒IC分类准确率为94.17%。

Conclusion: FedEDAuth有效验证了其在联邦学习中抵御拜占庭攻击的能力，为下一代硬件安全解决方案中的安全可信联邦学习框架奠定了基础，有望推动半导体供应链的鲁棒协作智能。

Abstract: The widespread of counterfeit integrated circuits (ICs) poses severe risks to the security, reliability, and trustworthiness of modern electronic systems. Federated learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving paradigm for collaborative counterfeit detection across the semiconductor supply chain, but its vulnerability to byzantine data poisoning attacks limits practical deployment. This paper presents Federated Embedding Distribution Authentication (FedEDAuth), a lightweight, embedding level client authentication framework that detects and filters malicious participants before model aggregation. FedEDAuth leverages reference embedding distributions derived from a golden dataset and evaluates clients using outlier analysis, mean shift measurements, and micro-cluster behavior without requiring access to raw data or gradients. Integrated into standard FL pipelines, FedEDAuth consistently identifies all poisoned clients in experimental settings with 50 distributed participants under the byzantine data poisoning attack, achieving a 100% malicious client detection rate. After filtering, the federated model achieved a high counterfeit IC classification performance of 94.17% accuracy. These results not only validate FedEDAuth's effectiveness but also underscore the broader potential of secure, trustworthy FL frameworks as a critical advancement for next generation hardware security solutions, enabling robust, collaborative intelligence across the semiconductor supply chain.

</details>


### [159] [PersonaFingerprint: Measuring Persona Inference on Modern Websites with LLM-Driven Browsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15962)
*Chuxu Song,Hao Wang,Richard Martin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现并量化了现代Web环境中的新隐私风险：攻击者仅通过加密流量元数据（数据包长度和到达时间间隔）即可推断用户的"角色"（persona）。作者构建了LLM驱动的多智能体浏览框架，在15种角色和10个网站上实现了约84%的角色推断准确率，并证明轻量级多任务学习可在保持高网站分类性能的同时提升角色识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网站指纹识别研究仅关注推断用户访问了哪个网站，忽略了现代Web场景中更细粒度的隐私风险——即攻击者可能通过加密流量元数据推断用户的角色/身份特征（如浏览行为模式）。

Method: 构建了一个LLM驱动的多智能体浏览框架，该框架通过可控制的角色约束条件，让计算机使用智能体与真实网站交互并收集对应的加密流量追踪。在封闭集和开放世界两种设置下形式化定义了角色指纹识别问题，并采用多任务学习目标来同时优化网站分类和角色识别性能。

Result: 在10个现代网站和15种角色（加上开放世界类别）上，混合网站流量的角色推断准确率约达84%；轻量级多任务目标可在保持约93%强网站分类性能的同时，将角色准确性提升至约80%。

Conclusion: 现代网站的加密流量元数据不仅泄露用户访问的网站信息，还可能泄露用户的浏览方式甚至身份信息，揭示了现有WFP模型中已嵌入角色信息且可以低成本放大的隐私风险。

Abstract: Website Fingerprinting (WFP) has traditionally focused on inferring which website a user visits from encrypted traffic metadata such as packet sizes and timing. In this paper, we identify and quantify a new privacy risk in modern web settings: an adversary can infer a user's persona using only packet-length and inter-arrival-time sequences. To study this risk at scale, we build an LLM-driven multi-agent browsing framework that enforces controllable persona constraints while a computer-use agent interacts with real websites and collects corresponding encrypted traffic traces. We formalize persona fingerprinting under both closed-set and open-world settings and further evaluate whether persona information is already embedded in representations learned by existing WFP models and can be amplified at low cost. Across 10 modern websites and 15 personas (plus an open-world class), persona inference achieves about 84% accuracy on mixed-site traffic; moreover, a lightweight multi-task objective can boost persona accuracy to around 80% while retaining strong site classification performance (about 93% baseline). Our results show that, on modern websites, encrypted traffic metadata can leak not only which site a user visits, but also how they browse and who is browsing.

</details>


### [160] [A Cross-Modal Prompt Injection Attack against Large Vision-Language Models with Image-Only Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16090)
*Hao Yang,Zhuo Ma,Yang Liu,Yilong Yang,Guancheng Wang,JianFeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CrossMPI的新型跨模态提示注入攻击，通过仅图像提示注入同时操控文本和视觉输入的模型解释，在多个LVLM和数据集上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入攻击存在关键局限：注入的提示仅能操控单一模态的模型解释，或虽为多模态但无法实现跨模态的提示扰动。需要一种能同时影响文本和视觉输入的跨模态攻击方法。

Method: 将注入提示扰动优化从视觉嵌入空间（10^5参数）转移到模型隐藏状态空间（10^7参数）；提出层级选择策略识别多模态集成关键层（发现中间层最优而非末层）；提出距离递减扰动预算分配策略，根据像素到语义关键区域的距离递减分配预算。

Result: 在多个LVLM和数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: CrossMPI实现了仅通过图像提示即可同时操控文本和视觉输入解释的跨模态攻击，为LVLM安全性研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for multimodal intelligence, but their growing deployment also expands the attack surface of prompt injection. Despite this growing concern, existing attacks still suffer from a critical limitation: the injected prompt for one modality only steers the model's interpretation of that singular input. Alternatively, these attacks remain multimodal but fail to achieve cross-modal prompt perturbation. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel cross-modal prompt injection attack CrossMPI, which can steer the model's interpretation of both textual and visual inputs via image-only prompt injection. Our design is underpinned by the following key breakthroughs. First, we turn the focus of the injected prompt perturbation optimization from the visual embedding space (typically with only $10^5$ parameters) to the model hidden state space (for multimodal information integration and with $10^7$ parameters). Then, two strategies are adopted to mitigate the optimization challenges posed by the larger parameter space. To constrain the optimized model parameter space, we introduce a layer selection strategy that identifies the layers most critical to multimodal integration. Interestingly, deviating from the past experience, our analysis reveals that the optimal layers for LVLM prompt perturbation reside in the middle of the model rather than the last. To constrain the image perturbation space, we propose a new distance-decremental perturbation budget assignment strategy that allocates budgets decrementally as the pixel distance to semantic-critical regions increases. Extensive experiments across multiple LVLMs and datasets show that our method significantly outperforms baseline approaches.

</details>


### [161] [PCDM: A Diffusion-Based Data Poisoning Attack Against Federated Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16098)
*Wei Sun,Yijun Chen,Bo Gao,Ke Xiong,Yuwei Wang,Pingyi Fan,Khaled Ben Letaief*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型（PCDM）的数据投毒框架，通过可调投毒向量和跳跃扩散策略，实现对联邦学习系统的高效、隐蔽攻击，实验表明该方法在攻击效果和隐蔽性上均优于现有GAN方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习因其分布式特性易受数据投毒攻击，现有基于GAN的投毒方法虽能生成看似合法的毒化数据，但GAN输出的固有一致性仍会暴露投毒痕迹，亟需更隐蔽且可控的投毒方法。

Method: 提出投毒导向条件扩散模型（PCDM），包含：(1) 可调投毒向量嵌入全局上下文实现细粒度毒化数据生成控制；(2) 跳跃扩散策略实现轻量高效生成；(3) 提供理论攻击性能保障。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100及无线数据集VRAI上，PCDM相比SOTA方法更不易出现统计异常，同时更有效地降低全局FL性能，能绕过包括拜占庭鲁棒聚合在内的多种防御机制。

Conclusion: 基于扩散模型的投毒方法对联邦学习数据安全构成重大威胁，其隐蔽性和有效性均优于现有GAN方案，需要设计更强的新型防御策略。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is vulnerable to data poisoning attacks due to its distributed nature. Although recent GAN-based data poisoning methods have indicated the potential of using generative AI to generate seemingly legitimate poisoned data, the inherent consistency of GAN outputs can still reveal a sign of data poisoning. In this paper, we propose a diffusion-based data poisoning framework against FL systems, which leverages a Poisoning-Oriented Conditional Diffusion Model (PCDM) to enable fine-grained control over the local generation of poisoned data while ensuring both attack effectiveness and stealthiness. Our PCDM incorporates an adjustable poisoning vector within the global context to precisely control the generation of poisoned data, with theoretical guarantees on attack performance. Furthermore, it employs a novel jumping diffusion strategy for lightweight and efficient poisoned data generation. We conduct the most systematic and broad experimental evaluation for FL poisoning attacks against various defenses, including advanced Byzantine robust aggregation mechanisms, on four open datasets: MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and a real-world wireless-specific dataset VRAI. Our results demonstrate that PCDM is less likely to exhibit statistical anomalies compared with the state-of-the-art methods while more effectively degrading global FL performance, which poses a significant risk to data security in FL.

</details>


### [162] [LymphNode: A Plug-and-Play Access Control Method for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16227)
*Hanyu Pei,Shang Liu,Zeyan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LymphNode 是一种新型的防御框架，通过在模型特征空间中注入广义稀疏通用对抗扰动（GSUAP），对未授权查询主动中和模型效用，同时为持有隐秘特征域凭证的授权输入选择性恢复功能，实现了类似免疫系统的“默认拒绝”安全策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方案存在根本性缺陷：被动水印仅作事后溯源，主动防御则带来过高延迟或需持续访问敏感训练数据。亟需一种实用化的后部署防御机制来应对边缘环境中模型面临的无限接口访问威胁（模型提取与逆向攻击）。

Method: 提出“默认拒绝”安全范式，通过向特征空间注入广义稀疏通用对抗扰动（GSUAP）主动阻断未授权查询的梯度估计与数据推理；仅对携带隐秘特征域凭证的授权输入恢复模型效用。该方法数据高效（<100样本，<1%训练数据）且支持跨数据集适配。

Result: 仅需不到100个样本（训练数据<1%）即可建立鲁棒防护；支持使用公开替代数据集实现跨数据集保护；在原始训练数据受限或不可用的高风险场景下实现轻量级、可即时部署的防御。

Conclusion: LymphNode 提供了一种实用、轻量且立即可部署的后部署防御方案，有效弥补了现有被动溯源与主动防御之间的空白，特别适用于训练数据受限的高风险边缘部署场景。

Abstract: Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) are high-value intellectual property (IP), yet deploying them to edge environments exposes them to \textbf{unrestricted oracle access}, rendering them vulnerable to model extraction and inversion attacks. Existing defenses fail to address this practically: passive watermarking only offers post-hoc provenance, while active defenses impose prohibitive latency or require persistent access to sensitive training data. To bridge this gap, we propose \textit{LymphNode}, a novel post-hoc defense framework that acts as an intrinsic ``immune system" within the model. \textit{LymphNode} enforces a strict ``default-deny'' policy: it actively neutralizes model utility for unauthorized queries via \textbf{Generalized Sparse Universal Adversarial Perturbations (GSUAP)} injected into the feature space, effectively blocking gradient estimation and data inference. Utility is selectively restored only for authorized inputs carrying a stealthy feature-domain credential. Our framework is highly practical: it is \textbf{data-efficient}, establishing robust protection with fewer than 100 samples ($<1\%$ of training data), and \textbf{cross-dataset adaptable}, enabling protection using public surrogate datasets. \textit{LymphNode} thus provides a lightweight, immediately deployable defense for high-stakes scenarios where original training data is restricted or unavailable.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Polymorphic Bottom-Up Weighted Relational Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15406)
*Dmitri Volkov*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在自底向上关系语言中实现多态性的新方法，无需单态化转换，通过将多态程序编译为基于等式模式和大规模实例的非多态程序，并证明了其正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自底向上关系语言中实现多态性而不依赖单态化（monomorphization），因为单态化可能导致代码膨胀和性能问题。

Method: 首先引入semiringKanren（一种自底向上加权关系编程语言），然后扩展其支持多态性；提出了一种基于等式模式（equality patterns）和大规模实例（large-enough instances）的编译方法，将多态程序转换为非多态程序。

Result: 成功实现了多态性支持，并证明了编译方法的正确性。

Conclusion: 该方法避免了单态化，为自底向上关系语言提供了高效的多态实现途径，并给出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: This work presents a new approach for implementing polymorphism for bottom-up relational languages, without monomorphization. We begin by introducing semiringKanren, a bottom-up weighted relational programming language. We extend this base language to support polymorphism. We describe a new method to compile polymorphic semiringKanren programs into non-polymorphic ones, based on equality patterns and large-enough instances of polymorphic relations. We prove the correctness of this method. Finally, we consider existing work and suggest directions for future research.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Kofola 1.0: A Modular Approach to ω-Regular Complementation and Inclusion Checking (Technical Report)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15390)
*Ondrej Alexaj,Vojtěch Havlena,Lukáš Holík,Ondřej Lengál,Yong Li,Nicolas Mazzocchi*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: Kofola是一个高效的Büchi自动机补集和包含检查工具，采用模块化补全框架，将输入自动机分解为强连通分量，并针对每个分量应用定制算法，同时提出新的在线空检测算法，在实证评估中显著优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Büchi自动机的补集和包含检查是自动机理论验证中的核心任务，应用于模型检验、监控和定理证明等领域，现有工具在这些任务上效率不够高或不够鲁棒。

Method: 采用模块化补全框架，将输入自动机分解为强连通分量(SCC)，为每个分量选择适合其结构性质的补全算法；基于此框架构建模块化包含检查，引入新的启发式方法；提出一种新的在线空检测算法，用于处理补全产生的广义Rabin对条件，允许在探索足够的态空间后立即终止搜索。

Result: Kofola在实证评估中与最先进的补全和包含检查工具相比极具竞争力，是最鲁棒的工具，在来自实际应用的基准测试上通常比竞争对手高出几个数量级。

Conclusion: 模块化的补全框架结合SCC分解和定制化算法，以及新的在线空检测启发式，能够显著提升Büchi自动机补集和包含检查的效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present Kofola, an efficient tool for complementation and inclusion checking of Büchi automata, two central tasks in automata-theoretic verification with applications in model checking, monitoring, and theorem proving. Kofola implements a state-of-the-art modular complementation framework that decomposes the input automaton into strongly connected components and applies to each component a complementation algorithm tailored to its structural properties. Building on this modular construction, Kofola also provides modular inclusion checking with new heuristics. A key ingredient is a new on-the-fly emptiness-checking algorithm for the simple generalized Rabin pair condition produced by our complementation, allowing the search to terminate as soon as the explored state space suffices. Empirical evaluation shows that Kofola is highly competitive with state-of-the-art complementation and inclusion-checking tools: it is the most robust tool in our evaluation and often outperforms competitors by several orders of magnitude on benchmarks from practical applications.

</details>


### [165] [Interpreting De Finetti's theorem in the Category of Integrable Cones (long version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15402)
*Crubillé Raphaëlle*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文建立了两个概率语义学结果之间的联系：Jacobs-Staton的范畴论版De Finetti定理与Melliès等人构造的线性逻辑自由指数（已在概率相干空间中实例化），通过随机核范畴与可积锥范畴之间的技术关联，进而刻画了概率相干空间!Bool中的全元素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示概率语义学中两个看似独立的重要构造（范畴论版De Finetti定理与线性逻辑自由指数）之间的深层结构联系，并利用这种联系解决概率相干空间中特定对象的表征问题。

Method: 建立随机核范畴与可积锥范畴之间的形式化关系，以此为桥梁连接Jacobs-Staton的范畴论De Finetti定理与Melliès等人的自由指数构造。

Result: 成功建立了上述两个构造之间的形式化联系，并给出了概率相干空间!Bool中全元素的刻画。

Conclusion: 随机核范畴与可积锥范畴的关系是连接概率语义学中De Finetti定理与线性逻辑自由指数构造的关键技术纽带，该连接可用于表征概率相干空间中的重要结构。

Abstract: We establish a connection between two results in the literature on probabilistic semantics: a formulation of De Finetti's theorem in the language of category theory due to Jacobs and Staton, and the generic construction of the free exponential of Linear Logic by Melliès et al, that has been instantiated in the model of probabilistic coherence spaces by Crubillé et al. The structural proximity of these two constructions is manifest, but making this connection formal requires technical developments on the relationship between the category of stochastic kernels and the category of integrable cones, two well-known categories in probabilistic semantics. We then use this connection to give a characterization of the total elements of the probabilistic coherence space !Bool.

</details>


### [166] [Understanding CDCL Solvers via Scalability Studies and Proofdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15506)
*Shimin Zhang,Yechuan Xia,Chunxiao Li,Jianwen Li,Moshe Y. Vardi,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模BMC实例（76,600+，766个家族）系统研究了CDCL SAT求解器的可扩展性，发现不同实例家族呈现线性、多项式或指数级扩展规律。之前的参数（子句-变量比、树宽、社区结构）无法区分线性与指数级场景，而新提出的"proofdoor"参数能有效解释这一现象。实验证明CDCL求解器能为线性扩展BMC实例计算小型proofdoor，而指数级实例的proofdoor呈指数增长且难以增量吸收，扰乱分支顺序会增大proofdoor尺寸并降低求解器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CDCL SAT求解器在实际工业公式中表现惊人地有效，但鲜有研究对其在工业实例上的可扩展性进行系统性的规模分析。现有参数（子句-变量比、树宽、社区结构）未能解释求解器在不同实例上的性能差异，因此需要新的理论框架来解释这一现象。

Method: 收集76,600+个BMC实例（766个家族）进行系统性规模研究；提出proofdoor概念（公式块之间的插值序列，代表求解器对已分析块推理记忆的备忘录）；通过实验验证CDCL求解器能否计算小型proofdoor、proofdoor的扩展规模与增量吸收特性、以及扰乱分支顺序对proofdoor尺寸的影响。

Result: 1) 传统参数（子句-变量比、树宽、社区结构）无法区分线性和指数级扩展场景；2) CDCL求解器能为线性扩展BMC实例计算小型proofdoor；3) 指数级扩展实例的采样proofdoor呈指数增长且通常不被增量吸收；4) 扰乱线性扩展实例会产生更大的proofdoor尺寸，关联到分支顺序劣化和求解性能下降。

Conclusion: Proofdoor参数能够有效解释CDCL SAT求解器在不同BMC实例上的可扩展性差异，为理解SAT求解器的实际性能表现提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Over the past several decades, CDCL SAT solvers have proven remarkably effective on large industrial formulas, despite SAT being NP-complete and widely believed to be intractable. While considerable empirical research has been done on solver performance over benchmarks like the SAT competition, as well as scaling studies on random and crafted families, surprisingly little effort has gone into systematic scaling studies over industrial instances. To address this gap, we collect a large benchmark of Bounded Model Checking (BMC) instances (76,600+ across 766 families) and perform a systematic scaling study of solver performance. We observe a spectrum: some families scale linearly, others polynomially or exponentially.
  Building on this foundation, we study the structural parameters that have been proposed to explain this phenomenon. We first show that previously proposed parameters -- clause-variable ratio, treewidth, and community structure -- fail to discriminate between the linear and exponential regimes. By contrast, the recently proposed \emph{proofdoor} parameter explains this phenomenon well. Informally, a proofdoor is a sequence of interpolants between chunks of a formula, where each interpolant represents the solver's memoization of reasoning effort on chunks it has already analyzed. In support of the proofdoor hypothesis, we make three key contributions. First, we empirically show that CDCL solvers do compute small proofdoors for linearly-scaling BMC instances. Second, we show that for exponentially-scaling instances, sampled proofdoors scale exponentially and are typically not incrementally absorbed. Third, we show that scrambling linearly-scaling instances yields larger proofdoor sizes relative to pre-scrambling, relating poor branching order to larger proofdoor sizes and drop in solver performance.

</details>


### [167] [Coalgebraic Non-Wellfounded Proofs: Recursiveness and GTC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15664)
*Mayuko Kori*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于余代数的非良基证明系统框架，通过余代数图上的全局迹条件（GTC）刻画可靠性，并利用递归余代数表征GTC，证明了满足GTC的余代数在伴随作用下的像为递归余代数时，存在唯一的余代数到代数态射保证可靠性。该框架在模态μ演算、高阶不动点逻辑及μ-双完备范畴中的非良基证明系统上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非良基证明系统的全局迹条件（GTC）是保证可靠性的全局性条件，但其非组合性与无穷证明结构的复杂性使得范畴化刻画极具挑战。现有研究缺乏统一的范畴框架来形式化GTC并建立其与可靠性的内在联系。

Method: 采用余代数方法建模：将推导树建模为预层上的广义多项式函子的余代数；利用余代数图表达无穷路径约束；通过递归余代数表征GTC；建立余代数到代数态射的唯一存在性条件。

Result: 证明了GTC等价于余代数在伴随作用下的像为递归余代数；在适当语义代数假设下，满足GTC的证明存在唯一的余代数到代数态射，从而保证可靠性。该框架成功应用于模态μ演算、高阶不动点逻辑及Santocanale循环证明系统的非良基变体。

Conclusion: 该工作为非良基证明系统提供了统一的余代数语义框架，将全局迹条件归约为递归余代数的标准概念，揭示了GTC与递归性之间的深层联系，为更多非良基逻辑系统的可靠性证明提供了新工具。

Abstract: Non-wellfounded proof systems impose a global condition called the global trace condition (GTC) on a derivation tree to ensure soundness. Providing a categorical characterisation of the GTC that guarantees soundness remains challenging due to the global, non-compositional nature of these conditions and the infinitary structure of non-wellfounded proofs. We develop a coalgebraic framework for non-wellfounded proof systems where derivation trees are modelled as coalgebras of generalised polynomial functors on presheaves. Since the GTC is a constraint on infinite paths in derivation graphs, we employ graphs of coalgebras and formulate the GTC coalgebraically as a condition on these graphs. Soundness is then formulated as the existence of a unique coalgebra-to-algebra morphism from a coalgebra representing a derivation graph to an algebra specifying semantics. Within this framework, we characterise the GTC via recursive coalgebras: a coalgebra satisfies the GTC if and only if its image under a suitable adjoint is recursive. Under an appropriate assumption on the given semantic algebra, this yields soundness, that is, every proof admits a unique coalgebra-to-algebra morphism. We demonstrate our framework through a non-wellfounded proof system for the modal mu-calculus, one for higher-order fixed-point logics, and a non-wellfounded variant of Santocanale's circular proof system in mu-bicomplete categories.

</details>


### [168] [Cut-Elimination for the Bimodal Logic GR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15732)
*Hirohiko Kushida*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于双模态逻辑GR的超矢列演算，其中两个模态分别表示哥德尔和罗塞尔的算术可证性谓词，并证明了该演算的切消定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为双模态逻辑GR建立形式化的证明论基础，特别是提供一个具有良好性质的超矢列演算，以满足哥德尔和罗塞尔可证性谓词的形式化推理需求。

Method: 构造了一个超矢列演算系统，该系统包含分别对应哥德尔和罗塞尔可证性谓词的两个模态算子，并证明该演算满足切消定理。

Result: 成功构建了GR逻辑的超矢列演算，并证明了该演算的切消定理成立。

Conclusion: 该工作为双模态逻辑GR提供了可靠的证明论基础，切消定理的成立确保了该演算的语法一致性和分析性证明性质。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a hypersequent calculus for bimodal logic GR, where the two modalities represent the arithmetic provability predicates of Goedel and Rosser, respectively. We prove the cut-elimination theorem for the calculus.

</details>


### [169] [LeanBET: Formally-verified surface area calculations in Lean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16169)
*Ejike D. Ugwuanyi,Colin T. Jones,John Velkey,Tyler R. Josephson*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文在Lean 4定理证明器中实现了完整、可执行且经过形式化验证的BET分析流程，涵盖窗口枚举、单调性检查、膝盖点选择和线性回归等步骤。在19条基准等温线上与BETSI参考方法对比，18条达到机器精度，仅UiO-66数据集有0.03%偏差，证明了形式化验证在科学计算工作流中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BET方法虽作为比表面积估算的标准工具，其实际实现涉及多个算法步骤，但这些步骤的正确性很少被显式证明。作者旨在构建一个可执行且经过形式化验证的BET分析流水线，填补算法实现缺乏严格正确性保证的空白。

Method: 在Lean 4中实现完整的BETSI风格工作流，包括窗口枚举、单调性检查、膝盖点选择和线性回归。采用多态共享实现同时支持浮点算术计算和实数域上的正确性证明。形式化证明了回归系数与规范定义一致且最小化最小二乘误差、BET线性化表达式的代数推导、窗口枚举的完备性和正确性，以及可接受性检查和膝盖点选择满足形式化规范。

Result: 在19条基准吸附等温线上与BETSI参考方法对比：18条等温线达到机器精度；UiO-66数据集偏差仅为0.03%。

Conclusion: 证明可以在Lean中构建科学计算工作流，既能提供形式化验证保证，又能与已建立的Python参考实现达到数值一致性。

Abstract: The Brunauer--Emmett--Teller (BET) method is a standard tool for estimating surface areas from adsorption isotherms, yet practical implementations involve multiple algorithmic steps whose correctness is rarely made explicit. In this work, we present a fully executable and formally verified BET analysis pipeline implemented in the Lean~4 theorem prover.
  Our formalization covers the complete BET Surface Identification (BETSI)-style workflow, including window enumeration, monotonicity checks, knee selection, and linear regression. We carry out computations in floating-point arithmetic and develop the corresponding correctness proofs over the real numbers, using a shared polymorphic implementation that supports both. On the proof side, we show that the regression coefficients returned by the algorithm agree with their specification-level definitions and minimize the least-squares error under the stated assumptions. We also formalize the algebraic derivation of the BET linearized expression and connect that result directly to the executable analysis pipeline. We further prove that the window enumeration is sound and complete, and that the admissibility checks and knee-based selection satisfy their formal specifications.
  We evaluate the implementation against the BETSI reference method on benchmark adsorption isotherms. Compared to BETSI, LeanBET agrees to machine precision for 18 of the 19 isotherms, with only a 0.03\% deviation for the UiO-66 dataset. This demonstrates that a scientific computing workflow can be built in Lean, yielding both formal verification guarantees and numerical agreement with an established Python reference implementation.

</details>
